## 5. Actionable rules (IF/THEN, with citations)

Rule ids are stable and are reused verbatim in the machine-readable block (§10). `WA1-NN` = PHẦN 1, `WA2-NN` = PHẦN 2, `WA3-NN` = PHẦN 3, `WA4-NN` = PHẦN 4.

### 5.1 PHẦN 1 rules (WA p4–65)

Actionable IF/THEN rules distilled from PHẦN 1. Every rule cites the printed book page. Vietnamese terms are as printed; the English label follows in brackets on first use. Ids `WA1-NN` are stable and are reused verbatim in BLOCK G.

**Ba quy luật [the three laws] (WA p26–40)**

- **WA1-01** — IF nguồn cung [supply] > nhu cầu [demand] THEN giá giảm (WA p26, p28).
- **WA1-02** — IF nhu cầu > nguồn cung THEN giá tăng (WA p26, p28).
- **WA1-03** — IF cung = cầu THEN "giá hầu như sẽ không thay đổi (hoặc biến động nhẹ)" — this is thị trường cân bằng [market equilibrium], where no pressure changes price or volume and price self-stabilises (WA p28, p30).
- **WA1-04** — IF a price change is observed THEN look for its cause — "một hành động nào đó không tự nhiên xảy ra"; the book's own example of a cause for a rise is supply thrown onto the market being absorbed ("Sự hấp thụ nguồn cung để đẩy giá lên") (WA p30).
- **WA1-05** — IF the cause (buying/selling built inside a phạm vi giao dịch [trading range]) is large THEN the target is larger; IF the cause is small THEN the target is lower — effect is "luôn có tỷ lệ thuận" with cause (WA p30, p32).
- **WA1-06** — IF price is breaking out of a phạm vi giao dịch THEN apply Nguyên nhân – kết quả to compute the target ("dùng để tính mục tiêu khi giá đang phá vỡ một phạm vi giao dịch") (WA p40); BUT the Point & Figure measurement itself is deferred "ở cuốn sách tiếp theo" — this book does not supply a P&F counting method, so do not derive one from it (WA p32).
- **WA1-07** — IF candle range and volume are proportionate bar by bar (low volume ↔ small range; higher volume ↔ larger range; volume higher than the prior two bars ↔ range higher than the prior two bars) THEN hài hoà [harmony] — expect the move to continue (WA p34, p36).
- **WA1-08** — IF candle range is very small but volume is very large, OR range is very large but volume is low THEN phân kỳ [divergence]: "nỗ lực tăng giá rõ ràng rất lớn nhưng kết quả đạt được lại rất thấp" (WA p35).
- **WA1-09** — IF, inside a rising sequence, candle 4 has a smaller range than candle 3 but "khối lượng tương ứng cây 4 lớn hơn khối lượng cây 3 rất nhiều" THEN phân kỳ → expect a reversal down immediately after ("ngay sau đó … giá đã đảo chiều giảm") (WA p36).
- **WA1-10** — IF, after a harmonious up-move, the following red candles show weak volume AND weak range THEN read them as "một đoạn hồi của xu hướng" [a pullback], not a reversal; expect the uptrend to resume (WA p38).
- **WA1-11** — IF price rises evenly while volume falls sharply THEN phân kỳ — "kết quả tăng nhưng nỗ lực không có"; the rise is easy only because supply is thin; this is the moment the uninformed FOMO-buy — do not (WA p38).
- **WA1-12** — IF, after the condition in WA1-11, red candles appear with increasing range AND increasing volume THEN supply is now high → "khả năng giá sẽ đảo chiều" (WA p38).
- **WA1-13** — IF an up-push with rising range and rising volume breaks kháng cự [resistance] THEN hài hoà (breakout accepted); IF the same push, with the same rising volume, fails to break kháng cự THEN phân kỳ (WA p39).
- **WA1-14** — IF volume is high (nỗ lực lớn) but range is narrow after a strong rise AND the close does not make a new high THEN "có thể sẽ dẫn đến việc thay đổi xu hướng tăng trong thời gian tới" (WA p39).
- **WA1-15** — IF effort is high but range is narrow after a strong decline OR the close fails to make a new low THEN "có thể dẫn tới việc thay đổi xu hướng giảm hiện tại" (WA p39).
- **WA1-16** — IF using Nỗ lực – kết quả THEN treat its output as an early warning of a possible trend change and as a short-horizon scenario only ("kịch bản của giá trong tương lai ngắn") (WA p33, p40).

**Cấu trúc thị trường [market structure] (WA p40–50)**

- **WA1-17** — IF price forms đáy mới cao hơn đáy cũ AND đỉnh mới cao hơn đỉnh cũ [higher lows and higher highs] THEN expect the uptrend to continue; the pullback lows are "những điểm lý tưởng để chúng ta có thể mở vị thế" (WA p41–42).
- **WA1-18** — IF price forms đỉnh sau thấp hơn đỉnh trước AND đáy sau thấp hơn đáy trước [lower highs and lower lows] THEN bearish structure; the pullbacks into new (lower) highs are "vị thế an toàn để chúng ta vào lệnh" (WA p44).
- **WA1-19** — IF a bullish structure breaks its prior swing high to a new high THEN mark it BMS [Break of Market Structure] and expect continuation; mirror for bearish structure at each new lower low (WA p42, p44).
- **WA1-20** — IF, in an uptrend, price forms đáy mới thấp hơn đáy cũ AND đỉnh mới thấp hơn đỉnh cũ THEN Failure Swing — "xu hướng tăng đã bị thất bại"; given context this "có thể gợi ý việc mở một vị thế bán ở đỉnh khi gãy cấu trúc sóng tăng" (WA p46, p50).
- **WA1-21** — IF, in a downtrend, price forms đỉnh sau cao hơn đỉnh cũ AND đáy sau cao hơn đáy cũ THEN Failure Swing — "xu hướng giảm đã thất bại" (WA p48, p50).
- **WA1-22** — IF confirming an uptrend Failure Swing THEN require the full sequence the book walks through: (1) a "thay đổi hành vi quan trọng" breaks the last higher low (đáy #N) and sets a provisional đáy #1'; (2) the next up-push is too weak to break the last high (đỉnh #N+1) and only tests it, setting đỉnh #1'; (3) price then breaks đáy #1' — only at (3) is "xu hướng tăng chính thức đã bị thất bại" (WA p47). Mirror (break of đỉnh #N → đỉnh #1'; weak dip → đáy #1'; break of đỉnh #1') for a downtrend (WA p49).
- **WA1-23** — IF judging whether an up-structure is durable THEN use đỉnh/đáy mạnh [strong swing highs/lows] or compare the length of the current impulse wave (sóng đẩy) to the previous impulse wave (WA p49).
- **WA1-24** — IF planning any entry THEN assess the overall market context first — "Việc đánh giá bối cảnh thị trường chung là quan trọng nhất" — to trace where large institutions have accumulated positions inside the trend (WA p49).
- **WA1-25** — IF the market is in củng cố [consolidation] THEN treat that as the ~70% base case and trend as the ~30% exception; price sits at an accepted value until an external, temporary factor reprices it, then returns to a new accepted level (WA p40–41).

**Thuyết đấu giá [Auction Market Theory] (WA p50–62)**

- **WA1-26** — IF buyers and sellers are both satisfied ("người mua nghĩ mình đã mua được … giá hời còn người bán cho rằng họ đã bán được … giá cao") THEN thời điểm giao dịch hiệu quả [efficient trading time] → price appears sideways as a trading range (WA p51).
- **WA1-27** — IF one side takes control THEN thời điểm giao dịch kém hiệu quả [inefficient trading time] → a trend that moves price from one balance zone to another; "điều này sẽ không duy trì lâu dài" (WA p50–52).
- **WA1-28** — IF an up-auction ends THEN a down-auction follows, and vice versa — trading is "một phiên đấu giá hai chiều" whose cycle repeats "mà không bị gián đoạn" (WA p52).
- **WA1-29** — IF new information creates disagreement between participants THEN "Thị trường hoạt động không hiệu quả" and begins to determine a new value (WA p53).
- **WA1-30** — IF price stays in a zone for long enough AND with large enough volume THEN that value is accepted; the operative identity is **Giá trị = Giá cả + Thời gian + Khối lượng** (WA p54, p56).
- **WA1-31** — IF neither side is comfortable at the current price THEN "họ sẽ từ chối thương lượng và giá sẽ di chuyển đến một vùng giá hấp dẫn hơn cho cả hai bên" (WA p55).
- **WA1-32** — IF a zone is fair value THEN price stays there longer (~70% of time in vùng cân bằng); IF a zone is imbalanced THEN price does not stay long (WA p55).
- **WA1-33** — IF a trend reaches a level where many traders want to open opposite positions THEN Giai đoạn dừng [stopping stage] — "biên trên và biên dưới của một vùng giao dịch mới được hình thành" (WA p59).
- **WA1-34** — IF price oscillates inside the newly formed balance boundaries THEN Giai đoạn đi ngang [sideways stage] (WA p59).
- **WA1-35** — IF price leaves the balance zone to seek new value THEN Giai đoạn chuyển tiếp [transition stage] — it "có thể di chuyển theo hướng tăng hoặc giảm không nhất thiết phải theo xu hướng trước đó"; do not assume prior-trend continuation (WA p59).
- **WA1-36** — IF chuyển tiếp completes THEN a wholly new cycle begins; the 4-stage process repeats on every timeframe, and "cả một quy trình 4 giai đoạn của khung thời gian nhỏ sẽ trong một giai đoạn của một khung thời gian lớn" (WA p60).
- **WA1-37** — IF you correctly identify xu hướng / dừng / đi ngang / chuyển tiếp THEN stop "bắt dao rơi" and "cản tàu", and map those four stages to the development of events from Phase A to Phase E inside the Wyckoff trading range (WA p60).
- **WA1-38** — IF asking where Wyckoff analysis applies THEN target the efficient (70%) sideways time — "Đây cũng là nơi tích lũy hoặc phân phối, là khu vực mà phương pháp luận Wyckoff hướng tới" (WA p53).

**Khối lượng [volume] (WA p4, p63–65)**

- **WA1-39** — IF CO / large players take a position THEN they "bắt buộc phải để lại khối lượng trên thị trường" — candles can be painted beautifully, volume cannot be faked ("không thể bị làm giả như việc vẽ nến") (WA p64–65).
- **WA1-40** — IF judging whether a breakout is real THEN do not rely on candle appearance and subjective comment alone; use volume / order-flow numbers (WA p64).
- **WA1-41** — IF building a trading system THEN volume is the indispensable element; "Trading mà không có khối lượng giống như khi bạn ra chiến trường mà không mang vũ khí" (WA p4, p65).

**Participant behaviour (WA p17–25)**

- **WA1-42** — IF Tích lũy Phase A THEN tay yếu bán tháo; tổ chức tay mạnh theo giá trị mua vào; tổ chức tay mạnh theo xu hướng đứng ngoài quan sát (WA p21).
- **WA1-43** — IF Tích lũy Phase D THEN tay yếu đứng ngoài; both tổ chức theo giá trị and tổ chức theo xu hướng buy (WA p21).
- **WA1-44** — IF Tái tích lũy Phase A THEN tổ chức theo giá trị chốt lời; tổ chức theo xu hướng mua thêm; tay yếu vẫn mua (WA p21).
- **WA1-45** — IF Tái tích lũy Phase D THEN tay yếu đứng ngoài; tổ chức theo giá trị "mua thêm một ít"; tổ chức theo xu hướng mua (WA p21).
- **WA1-46** — IF price is at the cycle top (Hình 2 points 7–8) THEN tổ chức theo giá trị distribute first (point 7: "bán (Phân phối)") and tổ chức theo xu hướng distribute one step later (point 8), while tay yếu buy at both points; on the way down (points 9, 11) trend institutions sell "(Ngắn hạn)" and value institutions are inactive until they buy "phạm vi thấp hơn" at point 13 (WA p20).
- **WA1-47** — IF the market falls sharply (Giai đoạn 1) THEN tay yếu "bán tháo bởi sự đầu hàng"; tay mạnh "gom hàng giá rẻ" (WA p24).
- **WA1-48** — IF price breaks upward (Giai đoạn 2) THEN tay mạnh (nhà giao dịch theo xu hướng) keep buying; tay yếu stand aside or sell what is left (WA p24).
- **WA1-49** — IF the uptrend is near its end with good news and influencers (Giai đoạn 3) THEN tay yếu FOMO-buy and new tay yếu enter; tay mạnh stand aside preparing "kế hoạch xả hàng" (WA p24).
- **WA1-50** — IF price is very high (Giai đoạn 4) THEN tay mạnh distribute by plan "không làm cho biến động giá thay đổi quá nhiều" so tay yếu keep buying — maximum quantity at the best price (WA p24).
- **WA1-51** — IF price breaks down into a downtrend (Giai đoạn 5) THEN tay mạnh (theo xu hướng) sell at the first sign; tay yếu still buy, believing it is only "một đợt củng cố" (WA p25).
- **WA1-52** — IF tay yếu are fearful / capitulating / leaving THEN tay mạnh are accumulating; IF tay yếu are euphoric with strong conviction THEN tay mạnh are distributing (WA p22).
- **WA1-53** — IF you accept you cannot buy the bottom / sell the top like CO THEN "mua ở đầu gối và bán ở cổ" [buy at the knee, sell at the neck] only "khi xác nhận được tín hiệu" (WA p17).
- **WA1-54** — IF reading Hình 1 THEN the "sao màu cam" points mark where CO gom hàng [accumulate] and the "sao màu xanh" points mark where CO xả hàng [distribute] (WA p18); the diagram itself prints red and blue/teal asterisks (WA p19) — see BLOCK F for the colour mismatch.

### 5.2 PHẦN 2 rules (WA p66–184)

- **WA2-01** IF three CHoBEV occur, each showing (a) reaction against the current trend, (b) increased spread, (c) increased effort/volume on the reversal reaction, THEN declare CHoCH: the prior trend is no longer maintained and a Trading Range may be drawn (WA p68).
- **WA2-02** IF CHoCH is confirmed after a decline, THEN draw the TR with lower border = start of the first up-wave and upper border = the point where price reversed after that up-wave (WA p69); in tích lũy the SC/ST lows and the AR high set the TR borders (WA p72); in phân phối the AR low is the TR's lower border and BCLX its upper (WA p102).
- **WA2-03** IF a CHoBEV or CHoCH is misidentified, THEN the whole phase labelling is wrong and trade decisions derived from it are wrong — treat CHoBEV/CHoCH as the first, gating step (WA p71).
- **WA2-04** IF you hold a profitable long and a notably large counter-trend reaction appears ("Phản ứng giảm lớn nhất trong xu hướng tăng"), THEN watch for two outcomes: consolidation (new TR) or a test of the highs followed by continued decline; do not assume the original trend continues (WA p70).
- **WA2-05** IF PS prints (wide-range bars, large volume after a long decline), THEN treat it only as the first hint of demand, NOT a reversal signal — start building the "price will stop falling" scenario, do not act (WA p73).
- **WA2-06** IF ST[A] holds above SC (most common case), THEN supply is drying up; IF ST[A] breaks below SC, THEN expect new lows or a prolonged consolidation with many further STs (WA p72, p74–75, p77).
- **WA2-07** IF ST[A] sits above 50% of the TR (AR–SC), THEN supply has thinned and the TR is likely to operate in its upper half (WA p75, p88); IF ST[A] sits below 50%, THEN supply remains abundant and strong fear points are expected later (WA p91, p97).
- **WA2-08** IF SC volume is low, THEN read it as either (a) supply absorbed all the way down (little volume possible) or (b) holders refusing to sell at a loss = latent supply; in either case expect a LONGER accumulation (WA p76).
- **WA2-09** IF price breaks above AR early in Phase B, THEN treat it as a bull trap (UA) where CO sells to clear stops above resistance; do not buy the breakout (WA p78, p88).
- **WA2-10** IF Phase B shows balanced tests at both borders with no unusual volume and no sign of CO, THEN do not trade — wait for Phase C (WA p95).
- **WA2-11** IF price breaks below TR support and quickly reverses to close back inside the TR on LOW volume (Spring), THEN this is a high-probability moment to open a partial long (WA p80); confirmation = the Test immediately after (supply check inside the TR) and price at least reaching the TR's upper border (WA p80, p153, p156).
- **WA2-12** IF most candles of the break close BELOW the lower border and price lingers there forming its own value area, THEN label Shakeout (supply remains), not Spring; expect a longer test and prefer dropping timeframe to find a local Spring[C]/LPS[C] (WA p80, p83, p93–94).
- **WA2-13** IF no Spring/Shakeout appears and price turns up from inside the TR (LPS[C]), THEN wait for a strong breakout candle or a small value area to provide the entry signal — candles alone cannot tell whether Phase B has ended (WA p82–83).
- **WA2-14** IF after Spring the price does NOT return to test SC support and rises with red candles showing long lower wicks, THEN CO is absorbing residual supply — LPS points on the way up are buy locations with excellent R:R (WA p89, p92).
- **WA2-15** IF price commits above the TR high with widening spread and steadily rising volume (SOS), THEN Phase D is in force; add to longs at LPS[D] pullbacks; IF price then holds briefly on the top of the TR wall (BU as a single LPS or a small structure), THEN the last supply test is complete (WA p84–85, p89, p92).
- **WA2-16** IF price exceeds the SOS high, THEN conclude the uptrend is officially formed; Phase E begins (WA p85).
- **WA2-17** IF a structure is being labelled at the SOS stage, THEN you are late — Wyckoff labelling value lies in Phase C (WA p77).
- **WA2-18** IF you are at a potential Shakeout[C]/UTAD on the higher timeframe, THEN drop to a lower timeframe: a local accumulation/distribution TR whose own Spring[C]/LPS[C] (or LPSY) agrees with the HTF context gives an earlier entry, tighter stop and better R:R (WA p93–96, p116–117).
- **WA2-19** IF you want to decide whether to hold or exit, THEN move UP a timeframe to see where the current TR sits inside the larger TR and use the larger TR's AR/SOS as the target (WA p98, p100, p120–122).
- **WA2-20** IF the higher-timeframe range shows a prior mSOW (strong supply) while the lower-timeframe range looks bullish at ST[B], THEN re-examine holding the long; the sell candidate is the point that is simultaneously LPSY of the larger TR, UTAD of the smaller TR and mSOW of the larger TR (WA p121–122).
- **WA2-21** IF ST[A] in distribution is above BCLX, THEN demand is still aggressive; IF below BCLX, THEN demand is fading — this sets how long the distribution range persists (WA p103, p122).
- **WA2-22** IF ST[B] pushes above BCLX resistance and quickly closes back below it (UT), THEN expect the next move to test the TR's lower border (WA p103).
- **WA2-23** IF price commits below the AR support in Phase B with increasing spread and volume (mSOW), THEN supply is dominant (WA p104).
- **WA2-24** IF a UT/UTAD prints, THEN a short has a very large R:R, BUT because strong hands use UTs repeatedly to stop shorts, it is usually SAFER to wait for Phase D and the LPSY confirmation (WA p106).
- **WA2-25** IF the rally after a supply test is weak and narrow-spread (LPSY), THEN demand is exhausted and CO is making its final sales — short at LPSY[C]/LPSY[D] (WA p107, p110, p147, p149, p177, p179).
- **WA2-26** IF price breaks the TR floor quickly with a volume spike (MSOW[D]) and the rally back is negligible, THEN the distribution is completing; further shorts at LPSY[D] retests (WA p109, p113, p179).
- **WA2-27** IF a lower-border break occurs in a distribution candidate, THEN prepare BOTH scenarios — Spring (it is really accumulation) and MSOW (it is distribution) — and let the reaction decide (WA p115).
- **WA2-28** IF the structure is a re-accumulation, THEN expect Phase A to look exactly like distribution (PSY, BCLX, AR, ST); do not short the BCLX on its own (WA p126–127).
- **WA2-29** IF volume falls progressively from Phase C to Phase E inside an uptrend pause, THEN the re-accumulation is effective — one of the most important tells (WA p130).
- **WA2-30** IF a re-accumulation shows higher highs with only LPS (no Spring) and jumps to SOS immediately, THEN it is the "sức mạnh" sub-type — the strongest; enter on LPS/Creek retests (WA p130–131).
- **WA2-31** IF a re-accumulation starts from a small local distribution (Local SC), THEN it is the weakest sub-type ("sau khi suy giảm"); absorption is often incomplete — do not force a buy (WA p136–137).
- **WA2-32** IF a re-accumulation is the 2nd, 3rd… in the trend, THEN the higher it sits the more likely CO switches to distribution — raise the bar for confirmation (WA p141).
- **WA2-33** IF a Phase B test in a re-distribution pushes above AR (UT[B]) or forms UTAD[C] or mSOW[B], THEN those are CO's best selling zones; LPSY[C]/LPSY[D] are the short entries that verify remaining supply (WA p143, p149).
- **WA2-34** IF ST[A] lies in the upper 1/3 of the TR, THEN support is very strong / demand strong — early sign of accumulation; IF ST[A] lies in the lower 1/3 or breaks the local SC support, THEN selling power is strong — early sign of distribution (WA p150, p166).
- **WA2-35** IF Phase B repeatedly tests the TOP of the structure (UA) and cannot reach the bottom, THEN expect an upward breakout from a mid-structure LPS; IF Phase B repeatedly tests the BOTTOM, THEN expect weakness and a potential LPSY as the Phase C test (WA p154, p157).
- **WA2-36** IF a Phase C shake is genuine, THEN price must at least reach the opposite side of the structure; ideally it breaks the structure — if it cannot, the shake is rejected (WA p153, p156, p160, p164).
- **WA2-37** IF the shake was only "một LPS đơn thuần" (no Spring), THEN rely on the prior structural bias and keep updating as information arrives (WA p161).
- **WA2-38** IF Phase D breakout candles show large volume + spread but price is NOT sustained and demand effort then fades, THEN it is a rejection back into the structure; open a short ONLY when aggressive selling negates the earlier weak demand (WA p163–164).
- **WA2-39** IF you are tempted to label mechanically at UT[B], THEN first read effort vs result there: UT[B] can also be a potential SOS; short only on divergence + rejection; likewise MSOW[B] can be a potential Spring — long only on exhaustion/absorption + rejection at the lower border (WA p166).
- **WA2-40** IF the cause and result are already fully developed, THEN there is no early trade left — you must enter before the result is complete, using effort–result to time the imbalance (WA p167).
- **WA2-41** IF a structure slopes, THEN rotate it mentally to horizontal: all events/phases still apply; only the controlling side into the Phase C test differs (WA p180–181).
- **WA2-42** IF you are a first-time Wyckoff operator, THEN use only horizontal schematics; the author explicitly does NOT encourage trading sloped patterns because the slope has no standard and the TR becomes subjective (WA p167, p170).
- **WA2-43** IF a rising-slope accumulation is in progress, THEN wait for the BU area where absorption commits long enough to show demand dominance (WA p170).
- **WA2-44** IF a falling-slope accumulation shows successive lows travelling shorter distances (SOT), THEN expect a failure to maintain the structure and a final supply/demand test before an upturn (WA p172–173).
- **WA2-45** IF a falling-slope distribution forms, THEN the Phase C test is the key to the downward break; the bigger the shock above the upper border, the stronger the reversal impulse; price will move very fast — hard to enter, but at least do not buy (WA p174–175).
- **WA2-46** IF price is still rising in what looks like a rising-slope distribution, THEN in Phase B it is only an assumption — wait for the Phase C action (UTAD) before treating it as distribution (WA p177, p179).
- **WA2-47** IF price bounces BEFORE reaching TR support, THEN demand is overpowering supply — expect an upward exit; IF price turns down BEFORE reaching resistance, THEN supply is overpowering — expect a downward exit (WA p182).
- **WA2-48** Stop placement (only explicit instance in these pages): BNB/USDT M5 long at 226.0 with stop at 220.0 just below the Shakeout/Spring low and target at the M30 SOS extreme 248.0 → R:R 1:4 (WA p96). Elsewhere the book states entries (Spring[C], LPS[C], LPS[D], ST[B], UTAD[C], LPSY[C], LPSY[D]) and targets (opposite border / HTF AR or SOS) but gives no further numeric stop rule.

### 5.3 PHẦN 3 rules (WA p185–220)

Rules are stated as the book states them; none of pp. 185–220 prints an explicit stop-loss placement rule — the only invalidation-type statements are WA3-08, WA3-18 and WA3-22.

- **WA3-01** — IF the range under study is phân phối THEN expect its phases to run faster than tích lũy and its volume to be larger and more massive throughout, with the biggest spikes at BCLX, PSY, UT, UTAD; look for entries "dựa theo hành động của CO" at those events. (WA p185–186, p194)
- **WA3-02** — IF the range is tích lũy THEN expect volume to fade from Phase C onward, spread thinly over weeks/months/years, with isolated spikes only at the key levels where CO gathers; do not demand distribution-scale volume from an accumulation. (WA p185–186)
- **WA3-03** — IF volume rises from Phase A and is highest in Phase C and D (hình thái 1) THEN read Phase C as the bull trap where tay mạnh sells the most, expect mSOW-type weakenings in B and rising supply committing price to support in D. (WA p191, p194)
- **WA3-04** — IF volume is heavy in A, lightest in B and heavy again in D (hình thái 2, "thường thấy nhất") THEN read Phase A as the main unloading site and B as a test; do not expect a Phase C bull trap — CO could not build one. (WA p192–194)
- **WA3-05** — IF Phase D volume is very large after price has already turned down THEN read it as CO forced to dump remaining stock at low prices because it could not sell at UTAD as hoped. (WA p193)
- **WA3-06** — IF price makes a UTAD AND you observe kiệt sức [exhaustion] and hấp thụ [absorption] supporting a decline THEN consider a Short there; low volume at the UTAD itself (few still interested) is consistent with the read, not against it. (WA p188)
- **WA3-07** — IF after MSOW a weak rally (LPSY[D]) is rejected at the Trading Range wall on large volume THEN, on a signal, enter Short — the book states CO is adding Short at LPSY[D]. (WA p189)
- **WA3-08** — IF a deep dip below the range occurs during Phase B THEN do NOT label it MSOW or Spring; the call is only possible after Phase C completes — wait for subsequent action and use modern tools to see which side dominates at that spot. (WA p189–190)
- **WA3-09** — IF a bull trap (UT[B]) is followed by a support break (mSOW) but price is held up again THEN it is not yet a downtrend; look for large-volume positions on the way back up as evidence of continued CO selling. (WA p188)
- **WA3-10** — IF a consolidation inside an up-move shows no extreme-demand sign, supply fading and demand entering (S<,D> or D<,D>S) THEN classify it hấp thụ ngang; expect slow absorption with final supply tests where sellers run out, then a push up — supply rises in A and B and fades through C. (WA p195–196)
- **WA3-11** — IF inside an uptrend supply increases and makes new lows, then demand increases sharply and price rises a lot and fast (S>>,D>>,D>S,S<) THEN classify it hấp thụ dọc; the fast recovery negates the prior supply effort and the next reaction is expected to be denied. (WA p195–196)
- **WA3-12** — IF a CHoCH has occurred AND out of the Spring supply is weakening with absorption on the way down on large-effort candles ("đỡ giá") AND then a sudden demand spike negates all prior supply THEN read nguồn cầu khẩn cấp: institutions entering a large position regardless of price with a long-horizon view — "Bắt đầu một chu kỳ giá mới". (WA p197–198)
- **WA3-13** — IF the absorption is slow (a wave or a Trading Range) THEN it is the absorption phase, not urgent demand; urgent demand is the abrupt spike that follows. (WA p197)
- **WA3-14** — Tích lũy Phase A: IF SC THEN expect CO to absorb all supply (support forms); IF AR THEN expect CO to sell part of the SC purchase to cap price (resistance forms) and trigger doubt-selling into ST. (WA p199–200)
- **WA3-15** — Tích lũy Phase B: IF ST THEN expect CO buying to test supply; IF UT THEN expect CO to sell to cap price even though the goal is accumulation ("tối ưu lợi nhuận"); from ST to UT CO buys only enough to keep price out of a downtrend. (WA p200, p203)
- **WA3-16** — Tích lũy Phase C: IF price falls from UT toward support THEN expect some CO selling to induce fear; IF Spring ("điểm sợ hãi thứ 2") THEN CO is the most active buyer, absorbs all supply and keeps buying on the way up to SOS. (WA p200–201, p203)
- **WA3-17** — Tích lũy Phase D: IF SOS THEN per the ledger CO sells a portion to re-test residual supply, forming BU/LPS "tùy thuộc vào nguồn cung hiện hữu", then buys on the way up; per the prose, after SOS CO sells nothing and a dip to support can only be CO's deliberate act — both statements are printed. (WA p201–203)
- **WA3-18** — Tích lũy Phase E: IF the structure is complete THEN CO is a net buyer/long-term holder and will not sell until price has risen significantly; a post-SOS decline all the way to support is therefore contrary to the plan unless CO intends it. (WA p202–204)
- **WA3-19** — Phân phối Phase A: IF BCLX THEN expect CO to sell a large block ("điểm sợ hãi đầu tiên", resistance); IF AR THEN expect CO to buy a small amount to support price (support). (WA p205)
- **WA3-20** — Phân phối Phase B: IF ST[A] breaks resistance THEN expect CO to sell into it and shake out Longs; IF mSOW THEN expect CO to buy a small amount to stop the fall — CO still buys support and sells resistance inside distribution "nhằm tối ưu hóa lợi nhuận". (WA p205)
- **WA3-21** — Phân phối Phase C/D: IF price commits above resistance again (UTAD) THEN expect CO to sell its largest block at that desired price and keep selling on the way down into MSOW; IF LPSY[D] THEN expect CO to open Shorts and add on the way down and again outside the range. (WA p205–206)
- **WA3-22** — IF MSOW has formed THEN CO no longer supports price; any subsequent rally rests on tay yếu only and should fail to make a new high / makes a lower LPSY — treat a strong, CO-backed rally after MSOW as against the plan. (WA p207–208)
- **WA3-23** — IF the distribution has a túp lều [tent] shape (straight up to a peak and straight back down) THEN expect CO to have dumped all supply in a very short time at a high price. (WA p206–207)
- **WA3-24** — Raincoat auction: IF supply is scarce, demand and supply both fall yet price rises ("easy move") THEN read SOS and commitment at the new level; IF many bidders chase the last unit(s) in an auction THEN read Buy Climax; IF supply rises (CO selling) into still-high demand at the top THEN read Buy Climax + CO's selling begins; IF CO keeps selling at lower prices as demand falls THEN read Thay đổi hành vi. (WA p210–213)
- **WA3-25** — (pp. 216–217) IF effort (volume) pushes up but price does not rise THEN the opposing force is supply; IF effort pushes down but price does not fall THEN the opposing force is demand; measure effort/result by chênh lệch and close, and check harmony especially when price crosses key support/resistance. (WA p216–217)
- **WA3-26** — (pp. 217, 220) IF KHỐI LƯỢNG >>> appears THEN decompose it as D>>> + S> (spike up) or S>>> + D> (spike down) and check whether the close is above or below the range; large volume is the sign that the opposing side has appeared — think supply first, then demand. (WA p217, p220)

### 5.4 PHẦN 4 rules (WA p221–293)

- **WA4-01** — IF uptrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume TĂNG ĐỀU (no abnormality) THEN effort/result harmonious, cầu tăng cung giảm → kịch bản TĂNG GIÁ, continuation (WA p221).
- **WA4-02** — IF uptrend context AND spread GIẢM AND volume TĂNG ĐỀU THEN supply is present to stop or reverse the rise → kịch bản GIẢM GIÁ (WA p222–223).
- **WA4-03** — IF uptrend context AND spread GIẢM AND volume GIẢM THEN demand is fading, supply flat-to-slightly-up → kịch bản GIẢM GIÁ (WA p224).
- **WA4-04** — IF uptrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume GIẢM THEN kịch bản TĂNG GIÁ but classify as *dễ bị tổn thương*: both demand and supply are thin, so any adequate opposing force can reverse it (WA p226–227).
- **WA4-05** — IF uptrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume TĂNG ĐỘT BIẾN THEN read as the appearance of phe đối lập / a climax / a stopping action → kịch bản GIẢM GIÁ, expect price to stop and enter a phạm vi giao dịch (tích lũy hoặc phân phối) (WA p228–229).
- **WA4-06** — IF downtrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume TĂNG ĐỀU THEN harmonious → kịch bản GIẢM GIÁ, continuation (WA p230–231).
- **WA4-07** — IF downtrend context AND spread GIẢM AND volume TĂNG ĐỀU THEN demand is present to stop or reverse the decline → kịch bản TĂNG GIÁ (WA p231–232).
- **WA4-08** — IF downtrend context AND spread GIẢM AND volume GIẢM THEN supply is fading, demand flat-to-slightly-up → kịch bản TĂNG GIÁ (WA p232–234).
- **WA4-09** — IF downtrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume GIẢM DẦN THEN kịch bản GIẢM GIÁ but *dễ bị tổn thương*: a sufficient demand force reverses it up (WA p234–236).
- **WA4-10** — IF downtrend context AND spread TĂNG AND volume TĂNG ĐỘT BIẾN THEN phe đối lập / climax / stopping action → kịch bản TĂNG GIÁ, expect a phạm vi giao dịch to form (WA p236–238).
- **WA4-11** — IF applying Bar by Bar THEN read only the *last* bar, and only to confirm a system signal / pursued pattern or when price approaches important support/resistance, looking for hài hòa vs phân kỳ — do not read every bar in isolation (WA p243).
- **WA4-12** — IF either context's Trường hợp 4 appears THEN treat the scenario as low-confidence: "chỉ cần một lực vừa đủ của phe đối lập cũng có thể làm giá đảo chiều không theo đúng kịch bản" (WA p243).
- **WA4-13** — IF reading candles sequentially THEN compare each bar's effort and result with the most recent bar of the same direction (`dEn` vs previous `dE`, `uEn` vs previous `uE`); same-direction effort AND result both larger → continuation call (Giảm / Tăng); effort larger but result smaller → "có thể dừng hành động"; both smaller → "khó có thể giảm sâu / khó tăng tiếp" (WA p238–242).
- **WA4-14** — IF an up-bar's effort and result exceed *all* prior up-bars in the range (as Nến 12: `uE12>uE4,uE7,uE10,uE11, uR12>uR3,uR4,uR7,uR10,uR11`) THEN treat the double-bottom (mô hình 2 đáy) as confirmed complete → Tăng (WA p240).
- **WA4-15** — IF a down-bar's effort and result are the largest in the range and negate all prior bars THEN large supply has appeared → kịch bản giảm giá (WA p242).
- **WA4-16** — IF a bar breaks an important resistance with large volume, effort up and result up (`uE1>, uR1>`) THEN kịch bản tăng giá; but IF the following up-bars show `uE<`, `uR<` versus that breakout bar THEN "chưa thể tăng" (WA p241).
- **WA4-17** — IF comparing analog bars THEN pair bars of the same role (Climactic Run, Change of Character, Selling Climax, Test of Climactic Action, Up Effort off the Test) across range 1 and range 2 and compare effort vs result; a divergence between the pair sets the bias for the current structure (WA p243–244).
- **WA4-18** — IF range-2 Change-of-Character / Selling-Climax / Test bars carry effort ≥ range-1 counterparts but weaker spread and close THEN that divergence supports a *phạm vi tăng giá* (bullish range) (WA p244–245).
- **WA4-19** — IF the range-2 Up-Effort-off-the-Test bar closes higher than range-1's with a large đuôi cầu THEN the supply test succeeded → count toward *phạm vi tích lũy tiềm năng* (WA p245).
- **WA4-20** — IF the five analog-bar comparisons all favour the buy side THEN classify the range as a potential accumulation (WA p245).
- **WA4-21** — IF an uptrend shows sign of supply → change-of-behaviour bar → nến cực đại → test of the climax low THEN the range's upper and lower boundaries are set; use this context read as the reason to exit and wait for the range to develop (WA p246–247).
- **WA4-22** — IF comparing swings THEN first fix the context (xu hướng vs phạm vi giao dịch), then score: phần mở rộng giá (%; S/R crossed), khối lượng (up/down, consistent), kết cấu (spread and volume variation), bối cảnh Wyckoff (phase / part of trend) (WA p247, p250).
- **WA4-23** — IF comparing down-swings THEN compare the two *primary* down-swings from range resistance, then the smaller *test* down-swings behind them (WA p248).
- **WA4-24** — IF the second primary decline extends less (−15.20% vs −16.60%) and fails to reach range support THEN selling force is decreasing → higher low → *phạm vi tăng giá* (WA p248).
- **WA4-25** — IF comparing swing effort THEN use the *average volume of the whole swing*; average(swing 2) < average(swing 1) means supply is decreasing — this alone does NOT prove accumulation; next locate demand (WA p248–249).
- **WA4-26** — IF the second decline shows no spread expansion where the first did THEN structure leans bullish; use kết cấu only for potential-trend inference once the range completes (WA p249).
- **WA4-27** — IF the secondary down-swing 2 has more down-effort than 1 but returns quickly into the range THEN treat as a potential Phase C low; look for a small range (Phase D) and LPS afterwards (WA p249).
- **WA4-28** — IF comparing up-swings THEN the most important comparison is the volume at the *start* of each wave, because smart buyers rarely keep buying once value is left behind (WA p250–251).
- **WA4-29** — IF wave-3's start shows even less demand than waves 1–2 while supply keeps decreasing across the structure THEN confirm uptrend readiness; IF Phase-B down-swings shrink successively THEN that confirms the bullish bias (WA p251).
- **WA4-30** — IF down-swing #1 > #2 in % AND `dE#1>dE#2, dR#1>dR#2` AND up-swing #1 < #2 in % AND `uE#1<uE#2, uR#1<uR#2` THEN the range is a *phạm vi tích lũy tiềm năng* (XAU/USD M5) (WA p252–253).
- **WA4-31** — IF the second decline pauses and a small Trading Range forms THEN take the Bar-by-Bar entry when price breaks that small range with a bar of large effort AND large result in harmony (WA p254).
- **WA4-32** — IF down-swing 2 extends more than down-swing 1 (0.43% vs 0.34%) but price commits below the range low and immediately re-enters THEN demand is present; combine with up-swing 2 > up-swing 1 (0.49% vs 0.40%) and higher up-effort → Trading Range tích lũy (AUD/USD M5) (WA p254–255).
- **WA4-33** — IF price starts to imbalance THEN the first zone to consider is the nearest previous balance zone; the longer price has been away from an accepted zone, the less it matters (WA p257).
- **WA4-34** — IF at VAH or VAL THEN expect support/resistance interaction (VA = 68.2% of volume, accepted; outside = 31.8%, rejected) (WA p259).
- **WA4-35** — IF the VA is wide THEN broad participation; IF narrow THEN little activity (WA p260).
- **WA4-36** — IF the profile extreme shows volume tapering off THEN completed auction (rejection); IF a high-volume block sits at the extreme THEN uncompleted auction — expect price to return there; when it returns, judge whether the intent is to finish and reverse or to continue (WA p260–261).
- **WA4-37** — IF planning an upside exit from a balance THEN require a *completed* auction at the balance's lower part; IF an uncompleted auction is there THEN discard the upside scenario until it is tested (WA p261).
- **WA4-38** — IF unsure whether an auction is completed THEN treat it as completed; uncompleted auctions must be obvious (abnormal cut in the profile, often at a VA limit) (WA p261–262).
- **WA4-39** — IF price is above the VPOC THEN buyers control → prefer Long; IF below THEN sellers control → prefer Short; avoid operating in the VPOC's immediate vicinity (WA p263).
- **WA4-40** — IF setting targets THEN use HVNs (past balance = magnet); IF seeking entries THEN use LVNs (past rejection = expected rejection, S/R) (WA p264–265).
- **WA4-41** — IF a trend is in progress THEN draw a Fixed Range profile over the whole move and prepare to act at its VPOC on the test; IF a Wyckoff structure is in progress THEN use Fixed Range to locate VPOC (control) and VAH/VAL and look for the test after the structure breaks (WA p266).
- **WA4-42** — IF several sessions' value areas overlap THEN build one Fixed Range profile over all of them instead of reading separate Session Profiles (WA p267).
- **WA4-43** — IF an uptrend pauses THEN look for a combination (pinbar, hai đáy, hai đỉnh…) in favour of the main trend at the VPOC; IF price is above the prior session's VA THEN the first continuation-test zone is the prior session's VAH (WA p268).
- **WA4-44** — IF setting macro bias THEN mark the LVNs and HVNs above and below current price on a variable/composite profile; use the longest-term HVNs as take-profit targets (WA p269).
- **WA4-45** — IF a large LVN plus a Shakeout sign appears in the macro context THEN consider a potential accumulation structure; IF an LVN sits close to the structure THEN account for a Spring appearing there (WA p269–270).
- **WA4-46** — IF a P-shape forms with the imbalance on the left (trend then range) THEN it can be exploited for the next trend; IF the imbalance is on the right (range then decline) THEN the opportunity is likely missed / it is a distribution; a P-shape after a long downtrend warns of the downtrend's end (WA p272–273).
- **WA4-47** — IF a B-shape forms after a long uptrend THEN it may signal the uptrend's end and possibly a new downtrend (WA p274).
- **WA4-48** — IF a D-shape forms THEN read temporary balance / institutions building positions before a break (WA p274).
- **WA4-49** — IF a structure's extremes are unclear THEN use VAH/VAL as the cause-building zone; the upper and lower limits of Wyckoff structures are always LVNs (WA p275).
- **WA4-50** — IF trading bias THEN always prefer the direction of the last HVN created; IF price is above an HVN THEN buyers control and a sell scenario is only considered once price crosses that zone from above to below (control changed); inside an HVN zone, do not commit to a direction until the break out of it is confirmed (WA p275–276).
- **WA4-51** — IF the new stopping point (đỉnh/đáy) travels a shorter distance than the previous one THEN momentum has fallen → SOT (WA p278).
- **WA4-52** — IF SOT is to be valid THEN require at least 3 pushes; 3–4 pushes are the useful window to look for it in the last push; IF more than 4 pushes THEN the trend may be too strong to oppose (WA p280, p284, p292).
- **WA4-53** — IF momentum falls but volume is large THEN divergence (effort large, result small): in a decline demand has appeared, in a rise supply has appeared (WA p281).
- **WA4-54** — IF momentum falls and volume also falls THEN exhaustion: in a decline supply is drying up (downtrend unsustainable), in a rise demand is drying up (uptrend unsustainable) (WA p282).
- **WA4-55** — IF SOT appears at a Wyckoff UTAD (three higher highs with shrinking amplitude on notably rising volume) THEN expect a strong decline to the lower TR boundary — a reasonable take-profit location (WA p282).
- **WA4-56** — IF SOT is seen THEN require a strong opposite push with large volume as confirmation, then wait for the pullback to support to join the new impulse (WA p284).
- **WA4-57** — IF SOT follows a potential Upthrust/UTAD THEN price may keep falling: be cautious with Longs, exit at the first weakness; IF SOT follows a potential Spring THEN be cautious with Shorts, exit on weak response (WA p284).
- **WA4-58** — IF SOT appears at the TR upper boundary signalling a decline THEN it has a higher success rate than an SOT in the middle of the range; a prior UTAD signal outranks a mid-range SOT (WA p285–286).
- **WA4-59** — IF three consecutive candles with shrinking range appear at an important reference (e.g. TR upper boundary) THEN treat as SOT in candle form and consider a sell if a clean signal follows (WA p286–287).
- **WA4-60** — IF impulse leg 3–4 exceeds 1–2 THEN strength; IF 5–6 is smaller than 3–4 THEN momentum weakened — but weakness does not mean price will fall; check for reversal or sideways (accumulation/distribution) (WA p288).
- **WA4-61** — IF holding a long and weakness appears THEN prepare two scenarios (stop + reversal signs; stop + sideways) and take partial profit on a notable change of behaviour; an entry needs a theoretical basis and an exit needs a reason (WA p289).
- **WA4-62** — IF pullback waves are small with weak volume THEN demand absorbs and the uptrend continues; IF a pullback becomes larger than the prior one THEN supply is entering — watch for change of behaviour/character, take partial profit (e.g. 50% or 30%), and IF UTAD or LPSY of distribution appears THEN close and Short (WA p291).

---

## 6. Patterns with identification criteria

### 6.1 PHẦN 1

Schematics and worked examples from pp. 4–65, with identification criteria. Numbers are only those the extraction parts record from the printed page; "~" marks values the extraction read approximately off a chart axis, not printed by the book.

**C-1. Hình 1 — "Wyckoff Logic / 4 pha của thị trường" schematic (WA p19)**
Identification: a hand-drawn price path with labelled turning points, red asterisks marking CO buy/accumulation points and blue/teal asterisks marking CO sell/distribution points, and a two-colour volume histogram beneath. Read left→right, the printed sequence is: **Cao trào bán** (red *) at the leftmost low → **Spring** (red *, with a nearby blue *) → **Bán tháo thất bại khối lượng thấp** (red *) with a horizontal line back to the Spring labelled **(Kiểm tra)** → a horizontal channel labelled **Tích lũy (Nguyên nhân)** → **Bán tháo khối lượng thấp** (blue *) at a swing high inside the channel → **Cú nhảy qua khe núi** (red *) breaking above the channel → **(Hiệu ứng)** / **Đánh dấu tăng** on the rise → **Cao trào mua** (blue *) → a top channel labelled **Phân phối (Nguyên Nhân)** → **Phục hồi thất bại** (blue *) with **Kiểm tra** → **Phục hồi khối lượng thấp** (blue *) → **Phá băng** (red *) breaking below the channel → **(Hiệu ứng)** / **Đánh dấu giảm** with the caption **PHÁ VỠ VỚI KHỐI LƯỢNG** on the final leg. Legend box: Tích lũy / Đánh dấu tăng (teal), Phân phối / Đánh dấu giảm (red). Volume bars broadly rise and fall with the swings, with one tall blue spike in the left third and one solid-black bar near middle-left (WA p19).

**C-2. Hình 2 — 13-point participant cycle (WA p20)**
Identification: a rising-then-falling "mountain" path in coloured blocks alternately labelled **Phase A** (pink/red) and **Phase D** (yellow) with **CHoCH** at each transition; circles 1–7 climb, 7 and 8 sit together at the peak, 9–13 descend. Each point carries a three-line behaviour label (tay yếu / tổ chức theo giá trị / tổ chức theo xu hướng); the full 13-line table is reproduced in §2.1 of the main file and summarised in rule WA1-46. Legend: Tay yếu (Tổ chức + Nhà đầu tư nhỏ lẻ) red; Tay mạnh các tổ chức theo (Giá trị) teal; Tay mạnh các tổ chức theo (Xu hướng) black (WA p20).

**C-3. Sine-wave distribution/accumulation diagram (WA p21)**
Identification: one hump (**KHU VỰC PHÂN PHỐI**, curve labelled **Giá mua**, two "X" marks at the inflection points) and one dip (**KHU VỰC TÍCH LŨY**, curve labelled **Giá bán**), joined by a blue **XU HƯỚNG TĂNG** arrow and a red **XU HƯỚNG GIẢM** arrow. Annotations: top-left "Dấu hiệu đầu tiên của phân phối thông minh bởi tay mạnh" / "Điểm phấn khích tột độ bởi những tay yếu"; top-right "Giá trị mua bởi tay yếu" / "Phân phối trên đường đi xuống bởi tay mạnh"; bottom-left "Điểm cực độ của sự sợ hãi và đầu hàng bởi những tay yếu" / "Dấu hiệu đầu tiên của sự tích luỹ thông minh bởi tay mạnh"; bottom-right "Tích luỹ bởi những tổ chức chấp nhận xu hướng sớm" / "Bán, giao dịch ngắn hạn hoặc không hoạt động do tay yếu" (WA p21).

**C-4. Paired tay mạnh vs tay yếu cycle diagrams (WA p23)**
Identification: two stacked dome/valley shapes, each with **Khu vực phân phối** at top and **Khu vực tích lũy** at bottom and the same blue/red trend arrows. Upper diagram, annotated "(Tình huống) hành vi tổ chức (Tay mạnh)": rising arm **Mua trong xu hướng tăng** → **không hoạt động**; top **Bán khi giá tăng**; falling arm **Bán bởi sự đầu hàng**; bottom **Mua khi giá giảm**. Lower diagram, "(Tình huống) hành vi tổ chức (Tay yếu)": the same five boxes (Mua trong xu hướng tăng, Không hoạt động, Mua khi giá giảm, Bán bởi sự đầu hàng, Bán khi giá tăng) — the contrast is which box sits where on the cycle for each hand (WA p23).

**C-5. Supply/demand icons and cross (WA p26, p29)**
Three balance scales (Cung left pan, Cầu right pan): level = **Giá ổn định**; Cầu side heavier = **Giá cả tăng**; Cung side heavier = **Giá cả giảm** (WA p26). Textbook cross: Price (vertical) × Quantity (horizontal); downward **Demand**, upward **Supply**; crossing labelled **Equilibrium** with guides to **P\*** and **Q\***; shaded **Surplus** above the crossing, **Shortage** below (WA p29).

**C-6. Point & Figure chart — $BTCUSD daily (WA p31)**
Identification: StockCharts.com P&F, "$BTCUSD Bitcoin to US Dollar CRYPT", daily, dated **23-Sep-2022, 07:34 ET**; printed header O **19,405.961**, H **19,500.17**, L **19,233.131**, C **19,277.32**, Chg **−128.641 (−0.66%)**, V **12606**; "No recent chart pattern found."; scaling **Percentage [Reversal 1, Box Size 2.0%]**; price scale with highlighted figures **17927.48** and the **19601.32 / 19611.32** area; volume histogram scale up to ~12.6K with **612.4K** noted atop one column. The page's prose is illegible (see BLOCK F). This chart is illustrative only — the P&F count method is deferred (WA p32).

**C-7. Vertical Chart vs Point and Figure Chart — Cause/Effect (WA p33)**
Identification: side-by-side panels. Left "Vertical Chart" (candles): pink box **Cause (Distribution)** over a topping range, yellow box **Cause (Accumulation)** over the re-basing range below, green arrow **Effect (Makeup)** on the subsequent rise. Right "Point and Figure Chart": same three zones; header "BTCUSD Bitcoin US Dollar", "P&P Pattern… **Double Top Reversal on 24 Dec 2021**", "Scaling Percentage [Reversal 1, Box Size …]" (box size not legible in the extraction) (WA p33).

**C-8. Single-candle hài hoà / phân kỳ (WA p34, p36)**
- p34: four teal candles rising in size with volume bars rising in proportion → **HÀI HOÀ** "Biên độ giá vừa và khối lượng phù hợp"; a separate candle with a small body and a very tall volume bar → **PHÂN KỲ** "Biên độ giá nhỏ và khối lượng lớn" (WA p34).
- p36 group A: candles 1→4 and volumes 1→4 rise together → **HÀI HOÀ** "Nỗ lực Khối lượng lớn ở thanh tăng giá với sự di chuyển tiếp diễn." Group B: candles 1–3 rise with rising volume; candle 4 is red, smaller-bodied than 3, with the tallest volume bar of the set → **PHÂN KỲ** "Nỗ lực Khối lượng lớn ở thanh tăng giá với sự di chuyển không tiếp diễn."; the arrow arcs up then back down after candle 4 (WA p36).

**C-9. Cluster / trend hài hoà vs phân kỳ (WA p37–38)**
- Left **HÀI HOÀ**: 4 rising blue candles then 2 red; volume rises across the 4 up-candles and falls across the 2 down-candles. Annotations "Lực đẩy biên độ tăng lên kèm theo sự gia tăng khối lượng" / "Đảo chiều xu hướng giảm kèm theo khối lượng giảm." Outcome: continuation (the red pair is a pullback) (WA p37–38).
- Right **PHÂN KỲ**: same shape, but volume is low/falling on the up-leg and rising on the 2 red candles. Annotations "Lực đẩy biên độ tăng lên kèm theo sự suy giảm khối lượng" / "Đảo chiều xu hướng giảm kèm theo khối lượng tăng." Outcome: reversal down (WA p37–38).
- Two volume-only bar groups A and B underneath repeat the same up/down volume relationships (WA p37).

**C-10. Resistance test hài hoà vs phân kỳ (WA p39)**
Left **HÀI HOÀ**: rising candles break through the horizontal red **Kháng cự** line, volume rising in proportion — "làm phá vỡ kháng cự". Right **PHÂN KỲ**: identical push with rising volume but price is rejected at the same line — "nhưng không phá vỡ kháng cự". Bar-only groups A (reaches a new high) and B (lower subsequent bars before a final rise) below (WA p39).

**C-11. "THỊ TRƯỜNG CÓ 3 HÌNH THÁI CHÍNH" schematic (WA p40)**
Three zig-zag panels labelled, as printed, "IN A CONSOLIDATION (Củng Cố)" (sideways), "IN A CONSOLIDATION (Xu Hướng Tăng)" (up), "IN A CONSOLIDATION (Xu Hướng Giảm)" (down), with **70%** under the sideways panel and **30%** spanning the two trend panels (WA p40).

**C-12. Bullish / bearish BMS structure diagrams (WA p42, p44)**
Bullish: ascending zig-zag, three successively higher swing highs each labelled **BMS** (underlined), two pullbacks, arrow continuing up (WA p42). Bearish: mirror — three successively lower swing highs labelled **BMS**, three down-legs, arrow continuing down (WA p44).

**C-13. Worked example — USD/JPY, D1, OANDA (WA p43–44)**
Chart header: O **138.920** H **139.355** L **137.924** C **138.233**, **−0.687 (−0.49%)**, Vol **73.689K**; date axis ~May → Nov of the following year; price axis ~107.500–152.500; annotations higher high / higher low / higher high / higher low / higher high (WA p43). Book's structure walk: đỉnh #1 in **130.000–131.000**; pullback ends at **126.000–127.000** = đáy #1; break of đỉnh #1 to a new high at **138.300** = đỉnh #2; short pullback ends at **130.000** = đáy #2; break of đỉnh #2 to **152.500** = đỉnh #3 (WA p44).

**C-14. Worked example — S&P 500, D1, OANDA (WA p45–46)**
Chart header: O **3666.8** H **3720.5** L **3578.0** C **3583.5**, **−83.3 (−2.27%)**, Vol **116.046K**; date axis ~Jul → 2023; price axis ~3200.0–5100.0; annotations Lower Low / Lower Highs / Lower Low / Lower High / Lower Low (WA p45). Book's structure walk: đáy #1 in **4100.0–4120.0**; rally ends at **4600.0–4620.0** = đỉnh #1; break of đáy #1 to a new low at **3600.0** = đáy #2; short rally ends at **4300.0** = đỉnh #2; break of đáy #2 to **3500.0** = đáy #3 (printed as "một mức cao mới ở (3500.0)" — see BLOCK F) (WA p46).

**C-15. Failure Swing Structure — uptrend (WA p46) and worked example WTI (WA p47)**
Diagram: four black up-legs (higher highs/lows) then a red lower high and lower low that break below a dashed diagonal trendline (WA p46). Example chart header "West Texas Oil, 1h, OANDA": O **71.631** H **71.638** L **71.346** C **71.561**, **−0.870 (−1.10%)**, Vol **242** (approx.); date axis 20, 23, 27, 30, Apr, 5, 8, 13, 17, 20, 24, 27, May; price axis ~65.000–82.000; caption **"Ví dụ: WTICO D1"** (timeframe mismatch flagged in BLOCK F). Book's walk: đỉnh #3 at **82.000**; pullback ends at **79.800** = đáy #3; break of đỉnh #3 to **84.000** = đỉnh #4; aggressive selling breaks đáy #3 and sets đáy #1' at **77.000**; weak demand only tests đỉnh #4 and ends at **79.800** = đỉnh #1'; sustained selling breaks đáy #1' → "xu hướng tăng chính thức đã bị thất bại" (WA p47).

**C-16. Failure Swing Structure — downtrend (WA p48) and worked example XAU/USD (WA p48–49)**
Diagram: red lower-highs/lower-lows zig-zag along a dashed down-trendline, then a black higher low and higher high that break up through the line (WA p48). Example chart header "Gold Spot / U.S. Dollar, 4h, OANDA": O **1956.505** H **1956.645** L **1949.705** C **1950.570**, **−5.935 (−0.30%)**, Vol **3.77K**; date axis Feb → Dec, 2023; price axis ~1500.000–2120.000; caption **"Ví dụ XAU/USD H4:"** (WA p48). Book's walk: đáy #3 at **1680.000**; rally ends at **1810.000** = đỉnh #3; break of đáy #3 to **1615.000** = đáy #4; aggressive buying breaks đỉnh #3 and sets đỉnh #1' at **1782.000**; weak supply only tests đáy #4 and ends at **1730.000** = đáy #1'; sustained buying breaks đỉnh #1' → "xu hướng giảm chính thức đã bị thất bại" (WA p48–49).

**C-17. Worked example — Bitcoin balance-to-balance move, late 2021 (WA p51)**
Balanced zone **58.000$–68.000$** through tháng 10 – tháng 11 năm 2021; late 11/2021 buyers refuse **68.000$**; on **4 tháng 12** a sharp drop seeks a new balance at **45.000$–52.000$**. Identification: the move between the two balance zones is the "thời điểm giao dịch kém hiệu quả" (WA p51).

**C-18. "THUYẾT THỊ TRƯỜNG ĐẤU GIÁ" cycle diagram (WA p53)**
Identification: **Người bán** (red) and **Người mua** (green) linked by two curved arrows (negotiation); side text "Thông tin mới tạo sự bất đồng giữa những người tham gia thị trường. Thị trường hoạt động không hiệu quả."; a larger loop down to **Thị trường bắt đầu xác định giá trị** and back (WA p53).

**C-19. BTC/USDT perpetual futures, 4h, Binance — efficient vs inefficient (WA p54, p56)**
p54 chart 1: a dotted-line trading-range box, a sharp drop, a second lower box; callouts **Thời điểm giao dịch hiệu quả** → the boxes, **Thời điểm giao dịch kém hiệu quả** → the drop. p54 chart 2: same instrument with labels **Thời gian** (top), **Giá cả** (right), **Khối lượng** (arrows to the histogram) illustrating the value formula (WA p54). p56 chart: header approx. O **24851** H **24850** L **24824** C **24824** (partly obscured); date axis 13, 17, 20, 24, 27, Tháng 11, 4, 8, 13, 17, 20, 24; price axis ~16600.0–21600.0; an extended upper range, a sharp vertical drop, a lower range; callouts **"Giá ở trong vùng cân bằng hơn 12 ngày"** (upper range) and **"Giá chỉ tồn tại 1 ngày ở trạng thái mất cân bằng"** (the drop) (WA p56).

**C-20. Bốn giai đoạn của thị trường diagram (WA p59–61)**
Identification: a candlestick chart (faint caption appears to be Bitcoin/Tether PERPETUAL FUTURES, Binance; date axis Tháng 6, Tháng 7, Tháng Tám) split by vertical lines into: a sharp decline (**1. Giai đoạn xu hướng**), a stop (**2. Giai đoạn dừng**), a horizontal range between two lines (**3. Giai đoạn đi ngang**), an upward breakout (**4. Giai đoạn chuyển tiếp**) (WA p59; enlarged on p60). A reduced line-chart version on p61 is split into **five** regions labelled "1. Dừng xu hướng trước đó", "2. Tích lũy nguyên nhân", "3. Đánh giá phe đối lập", "4. Khởi phát xu hướng" [không chắc: chữ rất nhỏ, có thể đọc là "Khởi phát xu hướng"], "5. Xác nhận xu hướng" (WA p61).

**C-21. Average daily trading volume bar chart (WA p63)**
Four bars: LONDON STOCK EXCHANGE **$7.2B**; TOKYO STOCK EXCHANGE **$18.9B**; NEW YORK STOCK EXCHANGE **$22.4B**; FOREX MARKET **$6.6 trillion** (tallest, label printed vertically inside the bar) (WA p63).

### 6.2 PHẦN 2

**P1 — Tích lũy sơ đồ #1 (with Spring/Test).** PS → SC → AR → ST(A) [Phase A] → (UA, ST in Phase B) → SPRING → Test [Phase C] → SOS → BU/LPS → LPS [Phase D] → Phase E; resistance = AR/UA highs, support = SC/ST lows (WA p71). Worked: **XAUUSD m15** — PS/SC 1732–1734 (SC volume < PS volume); AR 1742–1744; TR 1732–1744; ST[A] above 50%; UA 1744→1750 (resistance 1744–1750); ST[B] near SC; Spring buying zone 1726–1732 (điểm sợ hãi #2), no re-test of SC; LPS on the way up; SOS 1754 through 1744–1750; BU as LPS 1749 (WA p87–89).

**P2 — Tích lũy sơ đồ #2 (no Spring, LPS[C]).** PS → SC → AR → ST → ST in Phase B → LPS[C] → SOS → BU/LPS → LPS (WA p71, p82). Worked: **MATIC/USDT m5** — SC 0.770000–0.780000 (điểm sợ hãi #1); AR 0.817000; TR 0.770000–0.817000; ST[A] below 50%; UA 0.820000; "ST[B] as mSOW" 0.762000–0.780000 (điểm sợ hãi #2); LPS[C] 0.780000 with falling volume; LPS[D] 0.790000; SOS above 0.820000 confirmed 0.830000; BU as LPS[D] 0.810000 (WA p90–92).

**P3 — Phase C variants.** *Có Spring*: AR, ST(A), SC, ST(B), Spring, LPS, SOW (pair name `[không chắc]`, possibly NZD/USD) (WA p81). *Có Shakeout*: AR, ST(A), SC, UA, Shakeout (boxed), LPS, SOS with volume falling on each decline (WA p81). *Có LPS[C]*: PS, AR, ST(A), SC, ST(B), LPS with three declines on falling volume (WA p82). Identification: Spring = brief excursion below the TR closing back inside on low volume; Shakeout = most candles close below the border and price builds its own value there; LPS[C] = turn-up from inside the TR with no border break (WA p80, p83).

**P4 — Giảm khung (HTF Shakeout → LTF local accumulation).** Inset "Local accumulation as Spring?" with PS, AR, ST, SC, "UT Phase B", Spring (WA p93). Worked: **BNB/USDT M30** — SC 228.0, AR 243.0, ST[A] 234.0, balanced Phase B 228.0–243.0, Shakeout[C] toward 220.0–230.0 on maximal volume; **BNB/USDT M5** — labels AR, UA[B], ST[A], ST[B], SC, mSOS[D] `[không chắc]`, SPRING[C]; double-bottom break long 226.0, stop 220.0, target 248.0, R:R 1:4 (WA p94–96).

**P5 — Tăng khung.** Schematic PS, AR, ST, SC, JAC, BUEC, SOS, Spring, ST (WA p96). Worked: **USDJPY M30** (AR, UA, SOS, LPS ×2, SC, ST(A), ST(B)) — ST[A] below SC; điểm sợ hãi #1 at ST[B] with modest sell volume then heavy buying; entries for professionals at ST[B] and LPS; on H1 the position is a potential ST[A] with the H1 AR as target (WA p97–98).

**P6 — Phân phối có UTAD.** PSY → BCLX → AR → ST [A] → UT, mSOW/"SOW Phase B" [B] → UTAD → TEST [C] → LPSY → SOW/MSOW → LPSY [D] → Phase E (WA p101, p104, p106, p109, p111). Worked: **EUR/USD M5** — PSY 1.07150; BCLX 1.07220; AR 1.07200; ST[B] 1.07070 and 1.07060; UT[B] 1.07250 above 1.07200–1.07220; UTAD[C] 1.07300; LPSY[D] 1.07150; MSOW[D] to 1.06950; final 1.07040 (WA p112–113).

**P7 — Phân phối không có UTAD.** PSY, BCLX, ST, AR, UT, LPSY ×2, SOW Phase B, SOW (WA p101). Worked: **AUDUSD M15** — BCLX 0.67700; AR 0.67800; ST[A]; ST[B]–mSOW 0.67800–0.67219 with rising volume; failed mid-range bounce; LPSY[C] 0.67550 = short entry; two scenarios at the lower break (Spring vs MSOW) (WA p114–115).

**P8 — Giảm/tăng khung phân phối.** Inset around UTAD/Test (WA p116); "Tăng khung phân phối" schematic BC, ST, AR / BC, ST, AR / inset Upthrust, BC, ST, LPSY, ST, SOW, AR, SOW / ST, SOW, AR (WA p119). Worked: **USD/CAD M15** — recorded levels AR 1.36600, AR 1.37800, ST[A] 1.37000, UT[B] 1.38200, ST[B] 1.36600, UTAD[C] 1.38000–1.38600 (prose garbled) (WA p118–119); **USDCAD M15 → M30** — the M15 TR is a sub-range of the M30 TR; sell at the point that is LPSY of the M30 TR / UTAD of the M15 TR / mSOW of the M30 TR; target the M30 AR support; LPSY of Phase D as the entry (WA p121–122).

**P9 — Tái tích lũy sub-types.** (1) with Spring: BC, ST, AR / ST, Resistance, Creek, ST Area, LPS, Support, Jump across the creek / SoS, LPS; Spring on the dip (WA p126) — **WTICO M5**: PSY 87.500, BCLX 88.000, AR 87.200, ST[A] 88.100, Spring[C] 86.750–87.200, tests 87.200/87.600, SOS 87.300 (as recorded) (WA p128–130). (2) sức mạnh: BC, ST, AR / ST, Resistance, ST Area, Support / SoS, LPS / SoS, LPS, Jump across the creek (WA p130) — **NEAR/USDT H1**: PSY 12.700, BCLX 13.200, AR 11.900, ST[A] 13.500, resistance 13.200–13.500, ST[B] 12.600, MSOS[D] 14.000, LPSY[D] 13.200 (WA p132–133). (3) hấp thụ lâu hơn: BC, ST, AR / ST Area, Resistance, Creek, Support / SoS, LPS / SoS, LPS, Jump across the creek (WA p134) — **BNB/USDT H2**: PSY 150.00, BCLX 114.00, AR 114.00 as recorded (WA p135). (4) sau khi suy giảm: Small distribution pattern, BC, ST / AR, Local SC, ST Area, Resistance, Creek, Support / SoS, LPS (WA p136) — **AXS/USDT H4**: PSY 8.40, BCLX 12.00 `[không chắc]`, AR 6.60, SC 1.80, SOS 10.00, LPS[D] 8.20 (WA p139–140). Context chart: **WTICO 1H** three "Tái tích lũy" shelves 19.5–23.7 (WA p124).

**P10 — Tái phân phối.** Phase A (PS, AR, ST, SC) / Phase B (UT, ST(B)) / C–E (UTAD, Test, LPSY(D), mSOW, LPSY(D)) (WA p138, p142); no-UTAD variant LPSY(C), LPSY(D), mSOW, LPSY(D) (WA p147). Worked: **ETH/USDT M5** — ST[A] 1130.00; support 1112.00; FOMO push to 1140.00 (CO sells); SC 1122.00 or 1112.00; 1112.00 broken twice (ST[A], then MSOW[D]); LPSY[D] 1104.00 (WA p144–146). **GBP/USD M5** — ST[B] 1.15600; UTAD[C] 1.15600–1.15700; LPSY[C] 1.15100; MSOW[D] (WA p146–147). **WTICO M5** — labels Phase B, ST[B], LPSY(D), mSOW(D), UTAD(D); UT[B] 83.100 (WA p148).

**P11 — Dấu hiệu 1 (ST in 1/3).** Schematics SC, ST, PS, AR with a dashed 1/3 line, ST above vs below (WA p150). Worked: **MATIC M5** ST[A] 1.1300 in the upper third (WA p151); **SOL M5** AR 11.900, ST[A] at the lower border 11.550 (WA p152); **OP M5** SC as a local TR ~0.8700, AR 0.9050, potential Spring[C] (WA p152–153).

**P12 — Dấu hiệu 2 (Phase B test type).** Schematic Phase A (SC, ST, AR, PS, "X" low) → Phase B ending at UA (WA p154); distribution mirror Phase A (SC, PS, AR, ST as SOW), "X" (WA p157). Worked: **XRP M5** ST[A] 0.3800 (upper third), UA[B] 0.3840, LPS[C] 0.3700, Spring[C] 0.3680–0.3700 (WA p155–156); second pass ST[A] 0.3790, UA[B] 0.3850–0.3820 (WA p162); **NEAR M5** long LPS[C] 1.510–1.520 (WA p157); **NEAR M5** (bearish) ST 2.040, LPSY[C] 2.140, UA[B] 3.120–3.100, mSOW[B] 2.970, short 2.120–2.140 (WA p158–159).

**P13 — Dấu hiệu 3 (Phase C shake) + Dấu hiệu 4 (Phase D effort/result).** Schematics with "?" at Phase C/D and a declining volume histogram (WA p160, p163); PS, SC, ST, AR / LPS, UA / "X" / BUEC? JAC? (WA p161). Worked: **SOL/USDT M5** potential SOS[D] 12.300–12.700 rejected → short after aggressive selling (WA p164); **BTC/USDT M5** (axis ~15400–18000) and **LTC/USDT M5** (axis ~14.20–79.xx) charts, failed uptrend continuation → short (WA p165).

**P14 — Sloped variants.** *Tích lũy dốc lên*: SC, AR, ST (A) → UA → Spring, ST, JAC, BUEC; CHoCH ×2 (WA p169) — **LINK/USDT M5** (SC, AR, ST[A], UA[B], ST[B], SPRING[C]), **LTC/USDT M5** (SC, AR, UA[B], ST(B), LPS AREA (D), SPRING[C], mSOS[D]), no levels (WA p171). *Tích lũy dốc xuống*: AR, SC, ST → Spring, ST → UA, JAC, Creek; CHoCH ×2 (WA p172) — **LTC/USDT M5** (AR, SC, ST[A], ST[B], SPRING[C]), no levels (WA p173). *Phân phối dốc xuống*: BC, ST, AR, CHoCH, mSOW / UT, UTAD, ST / MSOW, CHoCH / ICE (WA p173) — **ETH/USDT M30**: BCLX 3360.00–3400.00, AR 3150.00, ST[A] 3300.00, ST[B] 3250.00, UTAD[C] 3360.00, LPSY[D] 3150.00–3200.00 (WA p175–177). *Phân phối dốc lên*: BC, ST, AR+CHoCH / ST(B) / UTAD, Test / ICE, mSOW / LPSY, SOW (WA p177) — **SAND/USDT M5**: BCLX 0.6000–0.6050, AR 0.5850, ST[A] 0.5950, UT[B] 0.6020–0.6050, UTAD[C] 0.6070, MSOW 0.5800, LPSY[D] 0.5970 (WA p178–179); **NEAR/USDT M5** (BCLX, ST[A], AR, ST[B], mSOW[C], LPSY[C], mSOW[D]), no levels (WA p180).

**P15 — Thất bại duy trì cấu trúc.** Three touches at each border, third marked "?" — price failing to reach a border before reversing signals the opposite side is overpowering (WA p181–182).

### 6.3 PHẦN 3

**P3-1 Distribution schematic with volume (WA p185).** Phase A–E; events BCLX (A high) → ST A → ST B (B low) → UTAD (C peak) → LPSY (D) → MSOW (D low) → LPSY (E, lower peak) → final low. Resistance line across BCLX/UTAD/LPSY highs; support line across ST-B/MSOW lows. Volume: blue histogram, several bars circled, a rightward arrow drawn across the bar tops (overall volume trend). Identification: unloading events carry the spikes.

**P3-2 Hình thái khối lượng 1 — rising volume (WA p190–191, p194).** Peaks A, B, C, D with C highest and D level with B; a red line near the base joining A and the trough before C; volume grouped A/B/C/D with a connecting trend line. Criteria: volume increases from A; highest in C and D; mSOW-type dips in B; Phase C bull trap where the most is sold; D commits to support.

**P3-3 Hình thái khối lượng 2 — A high / B low / D high (WA p192–194).** A/B/C/D zig-zag inside a red channel with a vertical line at D marking a sharp decline; triangle overlay labelled A, B, D with tall bars under A, short bars under B, mixed under D. Criteria: heaviest in A (main unloading), trough in B (testing), heavy again in D (forced dumping); no Phase C trap built. Stated as the most common form.

**P3-4 Worked example — USD/CAD M15 distribution (WA p187–189).** BCLX 1.37500–1.38000 (volume spike) → AR 1.36500 → ST[A] 1.37800 → UT[B] 1.38000–1.38200 (largest volume of the distribution, extreme candles) → minor SOW 1.36000 → two large-volume spots on the rally after mSOW → UTAD[C] 1.38000–1.38400 (light volume) → heavy volume down → MSOW[D] 1.35600 → LPSY[D] 1.36500–1.36700 (rejected, large volume) → Phase E decline. Decisions: consider Short at UTAD on exhaustion + absorption (p188); Short on rejection at the range wall / LPSY[D] on a signal (p189).

**P3-5 Worked example — AUD/USD H1 distribution (WA p192, p194).** Labels BCLX, AR, ST(B), UTAD, LPSY, SOW; ~3 weeks of bars (axis "19, 20, 21 … Nov … 10"); triangle A/B/D overlay drawn on the lower part of the chart. No price levels printed; overlay point labels not individually legible (p194).

**P3-6 Hấp thụ ngang [horizontal absorption] step (WA p195–196).** A sideways consolidation inside an uptrend: no extreme-demand sign, supply fading, demand entering (S<,D> or D<,D>S); supply up in A and B, fading through C; slow; final supply tests where sellers run out; then price pushed up. Chart instances: DOT/USDT M15 (two steps, p195), MATIC/Tether (one, p196).

**P3-7 Hấp thụ dọc [vertical absorption] step (WA p195–196).** Inside the trend leg: supply rises at high prices and makes new lows (sharp drop, "gây ra một số sợ hãi"), then a strong demand effort recovers price a lot and fast, negating the prior supply effort (S>>,D>>,D>S,S<); the next reaction is expected to be denied. Chart instances: DOT/USDT M15 (two, p195), MATIC/Tether (two, p196).

**P3-8 Nguồn cầu khẩn cấp [Urgent Demand] (WA p197–198).** Sequence: CHoCH → Spring → supply weakening → slow absorption on the way down on large-effort candles that hold price ("Cầu đỡ làm giá đừng giảm") → sudden demand spike negating all prior supply → SOS → BU/LPS. Chart instances: the "BSC/USDT (or similar futures pair)" chart labelled BCLX, ST/AR, SPRING ("CO gây gay mua vào"), SOS, BU/LPS (p197); S&P 500 Index V-reversal with a channel over the base (p198). No price levels printed.

**P3-9 CO accumulation ledger (WA p199–201).** SC +500k → AR −100k (400k) → ST +200k (600k) → UT −100k (500k) → Spring +200k (700k) → Spring→SOS +300k (1,000k at SOS) → SOS −200k (800k at BU/LPS; diagram label "BUC") → +200k on the way up (1,000k). Phase key: A dừng / B test A / C test A,B / D cung áp đảo trong TR / E cung áp đảo ngoài TR; CO roles: Mua/Bán, Mua/Bán, Thực mua, Mua ròng, Mua/Nắm giữ dài hạn.

**P3-10 CO distribution ledger (WA p204–206).** Diagram: +1000k → BC −500k (500k) → ST −200k (400k) → AR +100k (600k) → UT −100k ("Flat") → SOW +100k (500k) → UTAD −300k (200k) → MSOW → LPSY → Short selling −300k, −100k (−400k), −100k (−500k). Prose: BCLX −500k (500k) → AR +100k (600k) → ST[A] −200k (400k) → mSOW +100k (500k) → "bán tiếp 300.000" → MSOW → LPSY[D] short 300k → +100k short on the way down → +100k short outside the range = 500k short.

**P3-11 Túp lều [tent] distribution (WA p206–207).** Straight rise from a base to a peak and immediate fall back to the base; CO dumps all supply in a very short time at a high price.

**P3-12 Raincoat auction case (WA p208–214).** Supply 10 @ 1$. Hành vi 1: CO buys 7 (D=7↑, S=3↓) → Selling Climax / "lực bán cực đại", CO buying in Phase A–D. Hành vi 2: retail buys 2 @ 2$ (D=2↓, S=1↓) → easy move, SOS, commitment at 2$. Hành vi 3 TH1: 5 bidders for 1 @ 5$ (D=5↑, S=1↓) → Buy Climax; producer total 16$. TH2: 4 bidders, supply 7 (from CO) @ 5$ → Buy Climax + CO starts selling. Hành vi 4: CO sells 3 @ 3$ (D=3↓, S=3↓) → Thay đổi hành vi; CO accounting 7$ cost, 20$ + 9$ revenue, 22$ profit vs producer 16$.

**P3-13 Chênh lệch vs phạm vi thực bars (WA p218–220; boundary).** Chênh lệch = High − Low of the session; phạm vi thực = from the prior close to the next session's High/Low when a gap exists; gaps either "được che phủ" or "không được che phủ".

### 6.4 PHẦN 4

**P4-01 — Bar-by-Bar uptrend Case 1 (harmonious continuation).** ID: spread rising, volume rising evenly, no volume anomaly; `Vol > = D >> + S <`. Example: BTC/USDT Daily, price axis ~34000–65000, Jun–Oct, rising volume with rising range → continuation (WA p221–222).

**P4-02 — Bar-by-Bar uptrend Case 2 (absorption by supply).** ID: spread shrinking while volume keeps rising; `Vol >> = D >> + S >`. Example: GBP/USD H4, price axis ~1.29000–1.34000, reversal down (WA p222–223).

**P4-03 — Bar-by-Bar uptrend Case 3 (demand fading).** ID: spread and volume both shrinking; `Vol <= D < + S <=>`. Example: USD/CHF H4, reversal down (WA p224–225).

**P4-04 — Bar-by-Bar uptrend Case 4 (fragile rise).** ID: spread rising on falling volume; `Vol <= D < + S <`. Example printed "USD/UCAD M30" (WA p226–227).

**P4-05 — Bar-by-Bar uptrend Case 5 (buying climax / stop).** ID: spread rising on abnormally large volume; `Vol >>> = D >>> + S >>`. Example: Vàng M30, spike at the high → price stops and enters a range (WA p228–229).

**P4-06 — Bar-by-Bar downtrend Case 1.** ID: spread rising, volume rising evenly; `Vol > = S >> + D <`. Example: Dầu M30, continuation down (WA p230–231).

**P4-07 — Bar-by-Bar downtrend Case 2 (absorption by demand).** ID: spread shrinking, volume rising evenly; `Vol >> = S >> + D >`. Example: Dầu M30, reversal up (WA p231–232).

**P4-08 — Bar-by-Bar downtrend Case 3 (supply fading).** ID: spread and volume both shrinking; `Vol <= S < + D <=>`. Example: USD/CAD M15 (two charts), reversal up (WA p232–234).

**P4-09 — Bar-by-Bar downtrend Case 4 (fragile decline).** ID: spread rising, volume gradually falling; `Vol <= S < + D <`. Examples: USD/CAD chart with faint M5 header (WA p235); USD/CAD M15 (WA p236).

**P4-10 — Bar-by-Bar downtrend Case 5 (selling climax / stop).** ID: spread rising on abnormally large volume; `Vol >>> = S >>> + D >>`. Example: Gold M30 (faint header "Gold Root / U.S. Dollar, M30"), spike-reversal → range (WA p237–238).

**P4-11 — Gold M30 twelve-candle double-bottom confirmation.** ID: after the Case-5 spike, candles 1–12 read with dE/dR, uE/uR; demand tail on nến 5; nến 12 has the largest up-effort and up-result of all up-bars → "xác nhận hoàn thành mô hình 2 đáy". No price levels recorded (WA p238–240).

**P4-12 — Ten-candle resistance-break failure (Ví dụ 2).** ID: nến 1 breaks important resistance on large volume; nến 2–7 alternate with a bullish nến 7; nến 8 shrinking spread = sign of supply; nến 9–10 down-effort and down-result the largest in the range, negating all prior bars → bearish. Instrument not labelled; dashed S/R lines only (WA p240–242).

**P4-13 — Analog-bar two-range accumulation read.** ID: five role pairs across two consecutive ranges: Climactic Run (range-2 closes better, exceeds old high); Change of Character (range-1 effort ≥ range-2 but range-2 result weaker); Selling Climax (range-2 = two candles merged, effort ≥ range-1, spread and close weaker); Test of Climactic Action (range-2 effort ≥ range-1, spread smaller); Up Effort off the Test (range-2 closes higher with large demand tail) → potential accumulation. Instrument not labelled (WA p243–245).

**P4-14 — Context read: uptrend → range formation.** ID sequence: uptrend → sign of supply → change-of-behaviour bar → nến cực đại (stops the action, sets the range) → test bar of the climax low (confirms the stop, sets both boundaries). Diagram labels as listed in §4.2.1 (WA p246–247).

**P4-15 — Swing-by-Swing down-swing comparison in a range (Phase C candidate).** ID: two primary down-swings from resistance; chart labels "-274.75 (-5.81%) — 1000", "-401.000 (-9.03%) — 1604", "-777.70 (-16.60%) — 3111", "-714.25 (-15.20%) — 2957", "-446.75 (-10.37%) — 1787", "-373.75 (-8.27%) — 1855", "Volatility >>" / "Volatility <", "Phase [C]"; decline 2 (−15.20%) < decline 1 (−16.60%), average volume swing 2 < swing 1, no spread expansion in swing 2, secondary swing 2 re-enters range fast → potential Phase C, then a small range = potential Phase D with LPS. Instrument not recorded (WA p247–249).

**P4-16 — Up-swing comparison in Phase C (schematic).** ID: successive up-swings; wave-start volume compared; printed figures 17% (wave 1), 18.69% (wave 2), 16% (wave 3), "khu vực 18%"; Phase-B down-swings shrinking → uptrend confirmed (WA p250–251).

**P4-17 — XAU/USD M5 accumulation range.** ID: y-axis ~1638.000–1650.000, 10:30–17:30; down-swing #1 0.36% > #2 0.27% with `dE#1>dE#2, dR#1>dR#2` (annotation boxes "5.801(0.36%) 1988.1", "6.505(0.27%) 450.5"); up-swing #1 0.37% < #2 0.39% with `uE#1<uE#2, uR#1<uR#2` (boxes "6.009 (0.37%) 1600.5", "6.88 9 (0.39%) 695.5"); resolution: small TR after decline 2 broken by a large-effort/large-result bar (WA p252–254).

**P4-18 — AUD/USD M5 accumulation range.** ID: y-axis ~0.84400–0.85350; down-swing 1 0.34%, down-swing 2 0.43% (larger) but immediate re-entry after committing below the TR low; up-swing 1 0.40% < up-swing 2 0.49%, up-effort 1 < 2 (annotation boxes "-0.00219 | 0.34%|-258", "-0.00267 | -0.4392|-352") → TR tích lũy (WA p254–255).

**P4-19 — Volume Profile value area on BTC Perpetual Futures M5 (Binance).** ID: HIGH / VAH / VAL / HIGH labels; VA = 68.2% of volume (WA p259).

**P4-20 — Completed auction (ĐẤU GIÁ HOÀN THÀNH).** ID: volume tapering toward the profile extreme = rejection; BTC Perpetual M5 chart (WA p260).

**P4-21 — Uncompleted auction (ĐẤU GIÁ CHƯA HOÀN THÀNH).** ID: high-volume block at the profile end, often at a VA limit; WTI Light Sweet Crude Oil Future (SPOT) M5, y-axis ~75.000–91.900, two zones marked (WA p262).

**P4-22 — VPOC as control line.** ID: highest-volume price; chart with two VPOC labels on an instrument whose label the extraction could not read (WA p262–263).

**P4-23 — HVN / LVN boxes on BTC Perpetual M5.** ID: "HIGH VOLUME NODE" (upper, mid) and "LOW VOLUME NODE" boxes (WA p264–265).

**P4-24 — Fixed Range over a downtrend (WTI M5).** ID: box "ĐỘ MOVE GIẢM NÀY", VPOC arrow, y-axis ~76.480–93.350; price tests the downtrend's VPOC then forms a new downtrend (WA p266); second WTI M5 chart with VAH/VAL over a Trading-Range-like zone (WA p267).

**P4-25 — Session Profile continuation test.** ID: BTC Perpetual M5 chart with a boxed zone and a circled area of interest at the prior session's VAH / VPOC (WA p268–269).

**P4-26 — P-shape Profile.** ID: top-heavy profile after an up-imbalance then a rotation; chart label partly illegible (BTC-related, Binance, Ticks); price declines sharply after the profile peak (WA p271).

**P4-27 — B-shape Profile.** ID: bottom-heavy profile after a down-imbalance then a rotation; WTI Light Sweet Crude Oil Future (SPOT) M5, GARDA, price declines then recovers (WA p273).

**P4-28 — D-shape Profile.** ID: balanced two-sided distribution with a highlighted VPOC-like line; BTC Perpetual M5 (WA p274).

**P4-29 — SOT at a downtrend low (BTC/USDT Futures M5).** ID: strong down-wave, three wick rejections at 15950, 15900, 15850 with shrinking distance between lows; rising diagonal trendline under three touches; reversal after the third (WA p279).

**P4-30 — SOT at an uptrend high (Gold futures M5, COMEX).** ID: three new highs at 1777, 1778, 1779 with shrinking distance; sharp decline after (WA p279).

**P4-31 — SOT Case 1 schematic (divergence).** ID: impulsive decline, higher-low swings compressing between a red resistance line and black support line, upside breakout, rising volume (WA p281).

**P4-32 — SOT at UTAD (Wyckoff + SOT).** ID: three rounded higher tops at range resistance (circled), shrinking amplitude, notably rising volume; sharp drop to the lower TR boundary (WA p282).

**P4-33 — SOT Case 2 schematic (exhaustion).** ID: impulsive up-move, compression along support, three shrinking tops at resistance (red dots), breakout; volume declining then spiking (WA p283); real chart ("PERPETUAL FUTURES, 1S, Binance"-style label, unlabelled instrument) with three rounded tops and declining volume (WA p283).

**P4-34 — SOT after a potential UTAD, mid-range (failure case).** ID: schematic of up-move to resistance, three shrinking tops, dashed decline (WA p285); BTC/USDT Futures M5 — SOT mid-range after a potential UTAD; UTAD prevails, price falls to the lower TR boundary and continues (WA p285–286).

**P4-35 — SOT in candle form (Gold futures H1).** ID: at the TR upper boundary, three consecutive candles with shrinking range; consider a sell on a clean signal (WA p286–287).

**P4-36 — Impulse-leg amplitude comparison (pivots 1–6 / 1–4).** ID: labels "Khoảng cách giữa 3 & 4 tốt hơn khoảng cách 1 & 2 = sức mạnh", "Khoảng cách giữa 5 & 6 kém hơn khoảng cách 3 & 4 = suy yếu", "Khoảng cách quá độ giữa 2 & 3 tốt hơn khoảng cách 1 & 2 = sức mạnh", "Khoảng cách quá độ giữa 3 & 4 kém hơn khoảng cách 2 & 3 = suy yếu" (WA p288); pullback-wave variants on WA p290–291 carry `[không chắc]` label-mapping flags.
