## 8. Relationship to `knowledge/08-wyckoff-and-modern-tools.md`

Same author, other book. Status values: **NEW here** (absent from 08), **EXTENDED** (08 has a shorter version), **CONFLICTING/different** (both quoted, deliberately unresolved — a human must choose).

| Concept | Status | This book (WA) | knowledge/08 | Note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Ba quy luật — list and naming | EXTENDED | "Quy luật cung – cầu", "Quy luật nguyên nhân – kết quả", "Quy luật nỗ lực so với kết quả" (WA p26); boxed one-line uses of each (WA p40) | 08 §2.1 (WMT p015–016): Supply and Demand, Cause and Effect, Effort vs. Result | Same three laws; this book adds the explicit equations, the balance-scale/cross diagrams and the "dùng để" one-liners. |
| Cung – cầu — content | EXTENDED | Three equations "Cung > cầu = Giảm giá / Cung < cầu = Tăng giá / Nhu cầu = Cung = Giá hầu như sẽ không thay đổi" (WA p28); thị trường cân bằng definition (WA p30); Covid-mask example (WA p28) | 08 §2.1: "Wyckoff paid special attention to zones where supply or demand becomes exhausted, producing a major trend change" (WMT p015–016) | 08 stresses exhaustion zones; this book stresses the equilibrium state. Complementary, not conflicting. |
| Nguyên nhân – kết quả — measurement | EXTENDED (both defer P&F) | "để thấy được sự thay đổi về giá chúng ta phải tìm nguyên nhân" (WA p30); P&F "sẽ có riêng một chuyên đề … ở cuốn sách tiếp theo" (WA p32); "dùng để tính mục tiêu khi giá đang phá vỡ một phạm vi giao dịch" (WA p40) | 08 §2.1: cause = accumulation/distribution period, effect = subsequent move (WMT p015–016); no P&F counting method in 08 either | Neither ingested book teaches P&F target counting; 08 is not the "cuốn sách tiếp theo" for this purpose. |
| Nỗ lực – kết quả — hài hoà / phân kỳ | EXTENDED (08 goes further) | Candle-level, cluster-level and resistance-level hài hoà/phân kỳ diagrams and the two "lưu ý quan trọng" (WA p33–39) | 08 §2.1 (WMT p015–016) + §4.1 Effort-vs-Result rule with `E>`/`R>` notation (WMT p152–153) + Bar-by-Bar 10-case and Swing-by-Swing 4-case tables (WMT p159–213) | This book keeps the law at candle/cluster level and defers Tape Reading to its own PHẦN 4 (pp. 221–292, not covered); 08 supplies the bar-by-bar operational tables. |
| Nỗ lực – kết quả — the "lưu ý" wording | EXTENDED | "khối lượng giao dịch tăng cao (nỗ lực lớn) nhưng biên độ giá lại hẹp sau một quá trình tăng giá mạnh và giá đóng cửa không tạo ra một mức cao mới … có thể sẽ dẫn đến việc thay đổi xu hướng tăng" (WA p39) | 08 §4.2 Case 2: "strong ↑ volume, positive Delta, shrinking result → divergent (absorption) → Bearish" (WMT p161–201) | Same phenomenon; 08 adds the Delta column. |
| Price cycle — stage count | CONFLICTING/different | Five stages: "Giai đoạn 1" sharp decline … "Giai đoạn 5" downside break (WA p24–25) | 08 §2.2: four stages — "1. Sharp decline … 2. Price starts rising … late in the up-leg, good news appears and weak hands FOMO-buy … 3. Price high … 4. Price falls" (WMT p018) | Content matches almost line for line; 08's stage 2 merges this book's Giai đoạn 2 and Giai đoạn 3. Same author, different segmentation. |
| Strong hand / weak hand — definition | EXTENDED | Glossary: "Tay mạnh = Tổ chức + nhà đầu tư lớn mạnh; Tay yếu = Tổ chức + nhà đầu tư nhỏ lẻ" (WA p8); "Tay mạnh tương ứng với CO và các tổ chức lớn, tay yếu chính là những nhà đầu tư nhỏ lẻ thiếu kiến thức" (WA p22) | 08 §2.2: "strong hands (Composite Man / institutions)" vs "weak hands (uninformed retail, prone to FOMO)" (WMT p016–019) | Same dichotomy; this book's glossary explicitly puts some *tổ chức* on the tay yếu side, 08's synthesis does not. |
| Strong hands split: theo giá trị vs theo xu hướng | NEW here | "tay mạnh được chia làm hai loại khác nhau là tổ chức tay mạnh theo giá trị và tổ chức tay mạnh theo xu hướng" with phase-by-phase behaviour (WA p21); Hình 2 13-point table (WA p20) | absent | 08 has a single "strong hands" actor; the value-first-then-trend distribution ordering (Hình 2 points 7→8) is unique to this book. |
| Four-participant model (table WA p14–16) | NEW here | Tổ chức chống lại xu hướng (Giá trị / Khổng lồ / Vòng lặp vô hạn; CO, Warren Buffett, PIMCO, BlackRock), tổ chức theo xu hướng (Sự phát triển / Lớn / Theo chu kỳ; Quỹ hưu trí … Quỹ phòng hộ), nhà giao dịch chuyên nghiệp, nhà giao dịch nhỏ lẻ (WA p14–16, p25) | absent | 08 never enumerates participant classes. Table cell alignment is `[không chắc]` — see BLOCK F. |
| Four Wyckoff structures | EXTENDED | Tích lũy, Phân phối, Tái tích lũy, Tái phân phối — "4 hình thái chính" (WA p18), "4 cấu trúc chính" (WA p25) | 08 §2.5: Accumulation, Distribution, Reaccumulation, Redistribution (WMT p025–032) | Same four; 08 adds the "hardest to distinguish" caveat (WMT p236). |
| Five-item development list | EXTENDED (near-identical) | "Dừng xu hướng trước đó / Tích lũy nguyên nhân / Đánh giá phe đối lập / Bắt đầu thay đổi xu hướng [diagram: Khởi phát xu hướng] / Xác nhận xu hướng" (WA p60–61) | 08 §2.5: "(1) stop the prior trend, (2) accumulate the cause, (3) test the opposing side (Spring/Upthrust), (4) initiate the new trend, (5) confirm the trend" (WMT p026) | Same list; 08 names Spring/Upthrust as the item-3 test, this book does not name the event here. |
| Bốn giai đoạn của thị trường (xu hướng / dừng / đi ngang / chuyển tiếp) | NEW here | Four stage definitions and the bridge "4 giai đoạn trên chính là sự phát triển của các sự kiện từ Phase A đến Phase E" (WA p59–60) | absent as a named 4-stage taxonomy; 08 only has the 5-step list (WMT p026) | The Phase A→E mapping sentence exists only in this book. |
| Trading Range vs Cấu trúc thị trường | NEW here (BMS / Failure Swing) | BMS higher-high/higher-low structure (WA p42, p44); Failure Swing definitions (WA p46, p48, p50); four worked examples USD/JPY, S&P 500, WTI, XAU/USD (WA p43–49) | 08 §2.4 defines Trading Range as price oscillating between support and resistance, fractal (WMT p023–026); no BMS or Failure Swing vocabulary | 08 frames structure only as the range "box"; this book adds trend-structure and structure-break rules. |
| Time-in-range figure | CONFLICTING/different | "Giai đoạn củng cố thông thường sẽ chiếm khoảng 70% thời gian trên thị trường còn hình thái có xu hướng sẽ chiếm khoảng 30%" (WA p41); "thời gian giao dịch hiệu quả chiếm tới 70%" (WA p52); "khoảng 70% thời gian" (WA p55) | 08 §2.4: "markets spend **70–80% of their time ranging**" citing Dalton (WMT p232) | 70% flat vs 70–80% band. Not resolved here. |
| Fractality | EXTENDED | "Quy trình gồm 4 giai đoạn như trên sẽ liên tục diễn ra trên thị trường ở mọi khung thời gian khác nhau" (WA p60) | 08 §2.4: "Trading Ranges nest inside larger ones across timeframes" (WMT p023–026) | Same claim, stated on stages here and on ranges in 08. |
| Auction theory — attribution | CONFLICTING/different | "J. Peter Steidlmayer là người đầu tiên phát triển Market Profile … Jim Dalton … trong cuốn sách **Mind Over the Market**" (WA p50) | 08 §2.4: "Citing James F. Dalton's *Mind Over Markets* (explicitly named, WMT p232)"; 08 §3.1: "James Dalton, Robert Bevan Dalton, Eric Jones, *Markets in Profile*" (WMT p070) | Book title printed as "Mind Over the Market" here vs "Mind Over Markets" in 08; Steidlmayer is credited only here. Not resolved. |
| Auction theory — content | EXTENDED | Three main ideas (WA p50); efficient vs inefficient time (WA p51–52); two-way auction (WA p52); **Giá trị = Giá cả + Thời gian + Khối lượng** (WA p54) | 08 §3.1: "an auction is composed of price, time, and volume … Value Area … reversion vs trend-following" (WMT p069–079) | Same three components; 08 operationalises them into VAH/VAL/LVN/VPOC, which this book's PHẦN 1 does not mention. |

| SOT definition | EXTENDED | WA p278: "điểm dừng mới (đỉnh/đáy) có biên độ ngắn hơn điểm dừng cũ"; "rút ngắn lực đẩy"; acronym not expanded | 08 §2.3 (WMT p019–022): "progressively shorter/shallower price swings … especially failure of a third push"; acronym never expanded | Same concept, both unexpanded. |
| SOT push count | CONFLICTING/different | WA p280, p284, p292: minimum 3 pushes; 3–4 useful; **more than 4 → trend too strong to oppose** | 08 §2.3: "especially failure of a third push" — no upper bound stated | WA adds the >4 exclusion. |
| SOT × volume (divergence vs exhaustion) | EXTENDED | WA p281–283, p292: Trường hợp 1 (large volume = divergence) / Trường hợp 2 (falling volume = kiệt sức) | 08 §2.3: SOT with increasing volume = emerging demand; with decreasing volume = pushing side running out | Same two-way logic; WA names them phân kỳ / kiệt sức. |
| SOT location vs Wyckoff events | NEW here | WA p282, p284–286: SOT at UTAD; SOT after UTAD/Spring caution; TR-edge SOT > mid-range SOT; UTAD outranks SOT | 08 §5 Step 6 (WMT p273–274): SOT at the opposite range edge used as an exit trigger | WA governs entry/priority; 08 uses it for exits. |
| SOT in candle form | NEW here | WA p286–287 (Gold futures H1) | absent | — |
| Impulse/pullback leg comparison, partial TP 50%/30%, UTAD/LPSY exit | NEW here | WA p287–291 | absent (08 §5 Step 6 uses breakeven/trailing, no partial-size figures) | — |
| SOT worked examples | NEW here | WA p279: BTC/USDT Futures M5 (15950/15900/15850), Gold COMEX M5 (1777/1778/1779) | 08: SOT appears inside case-study exits (WMT p252, p262, p273, p276, p287, p304), no standalone numeric example | — |

## 9. Flags, ambiguities and gaps inside this source

Every `[không chắc: …]` flag raised by an extraction pass is preserved here verbatim and deliberately **not** resolved.

### 9.1 PHẦN 1

**F-1. `[không chắc: …]` flags carried verbatim from the extraction parts (pp. 4–65)**

- WA p13 — `[không chắc: chữ "để cập" — có thể là lỗi in ấn của "đề cập"; ghi lại nguyên văn như in trong sách.]` (on the FOMO footnote).
- WA p15 — `[không chắc: hàng đầu bảng lặp lại tiêu đề "Quỹ hưu trí"/"Công ty bảo hiểm" ở các dòng dưới, xem dưới]` (row 1 of the trend-following institutions table).
- WA p15 — `[không chắc: việc ghép chính xác từng ô nội dung vào đúng cột trong bảng xoay/nén này có một phần không chắc chắn do bảng bị chia cắt qua nhiều ảnh chụp; đã ghi lại đầy đủ mọi nhãn/nội dung nhìn thấy trên trang theo đúng thứ tự đọc trái→phải, trên→dưới. Đại diện xuất hiện trên trang: Quỹ hưu trí, Công ty bảo hiểm, Ngân hàng đầu tư, Quỹ tương hỗ, Quỹ phòng hộ.]`
- WA p16 — `[không chắc: đây là bảng rất dày đặc, xoay và bị ngón tay che một phần; đã ghi lại mọi nhãn đọc được theo đúng thứ tự trái→phải/trên→dưới trên trang. Hai nhóm đối tượng chính trên trang này là "Những nhà giao dịch chuyên nghiệp" và "Nhà giao dịch nhỏ lẻ", đối lập với "Những nhà đầu tư thuộc tổ chức" ở hai trang trước.]`

**F-2. Illegible / missing pages, exactly as the parts describe them**

- **pp. 1–3** — not photographed. Part-01 coverage note: "book pages 1–3 are not covered by any photo in this range (front matter — title page / copyright / possible blank leaf — was not photographed; content picks up mid-'LỜI NÓI ĐẦU' at page 4 in photo 002)". The LỜI NÓI ĐẦU heading on p4 "continues from a preceding unphotographed page".

- **p31** — "Photo was rotated 90°; original orientation is landscape (chart occupies most of page, with paragraph text faintly visible in background/bleed-through, unreadable …)"; "No transcribable prose text could be confidently read on this photo; the page-31 body text is present only as faint mirrored bleed-through from the facing page and is not legible." Only the $BTCUSD P&F chart and its header data were read.

- **p54, p56** — BTC/USDT chart headers "partly obscured"; p56 OHLC recorded as "approximate, partly obscured".

Part-02 ends at book p56 (photo 054) and part-03 begins at book p59 (photo 057); the two pages between "Khối lượng" under Sự quan trọng của giá trị (p56) and "BỐN GIAI ĐOẠN CỦA THỊ TRƯỜNG" (p59) are absent. Any content there (e.g. a transition from the value formula to the four stages) is unknown.

**F-3. Internal inconsistencies noticed between pages (both sides quoted)**

- **Star colours, Hình 1.** Text: "ở những điểm sao màu cam là những điểm … lý tưởng để CO có thể gom hàng. Ở những dấu sao màu xanh là những điểm … để CO có thể xả hàng" (WA p18). Diagram as extracted: "red asterisks (*) marking CO buy/accumulation-related points and blue/teal asterisks (*) marking CO sell/distribution-related points" — cam vs red may be the same ink; but the diagram also prints **Phá băng** (the breakdown out of distribution) with a **red** * and gives **Spring** both a red * and "a nearby blue *" (WA p19), which does not follow the p18 colour rule as stated.
- **"Bán tháo" at a swing high, Hình 1.** The label **Bán tháo khối lượng thấp** is placed at "a swing high inside the channel" of the accumulation range (WA p19), whereas "bán tháo" elsewhere in PHẦN 1 denotes capitulation selling at lows (WA p24). Recorded as printed.
- **Who is "tay yếu".** Glossary: "Tay yếu — Tổ chức + nhà đầu tư nhỏ lẻ" (WA p8). Later: "tay yếu chính là những nhà đầu tư nhỏ lẻ thiếu kiến thức" (WA p22). And: "có hai loại tổ chức là tay mạnh và tay yếu" (WA p21) — the glossary includes organisations in tay yếu, p22 does not.
- **"Hình thái chính" used for two different lists.** "Theo phương pháp luận Wyckoff ta sẽ có 4 hình thái chính của thị trường đó là: Tích lũy / Phân phối / Tái tích lũy / Tái phân phối" (WA p18) vs "THỊ TRƯỜNG CÓ 3 HÌNH THÁI CHÍNH … củng cố, xu hướng tăng và xu hướng giảm" (WA p40). The p25 summary re-labels the first list "4 cấu trúc chính".
- **Diagram labels, WA p40.** All three panels are captioned "IN A CONSOLIDATION (…)", including "(Xu Hướng Tăng)" and "(Xu Hướng Giảm)", which by the body text are not consolidation.
- **"Giai đoạn" means two different taxonomies.** "Giai đoạn 1 … Giai đoạn 5" of the participant price cycle (WA p24–25) vs "BỐN GIAI ĐOẠN CỦA THỊ TRƯỜNG" xu hướng / dừng / đi ngang / chuyển tiếp (WA p59). Neither page cross-references the other.
- **Loss-rate figures.** "có hơn 90% nhà giao dịch không quan tâm đến khối lượng giao dịch hoặc hiểu chưa đúng" (WA p4) vs "đây cũng chính là điều khiến 99% trader trên thị trường thua lỗ" (WA p22). Different claims (ignore volume vs lose), both unsourced.
- **S&P 500 wording.** "Hành động giá tiếp theo phá vỡ đáy #2 tạo ra một mức cao mới ở (3500.0) tạo ra đáy thứ #3" (WA p46) — a "mức cao mới" [new high] paired with a lower low at 3500.0 in a downtrend walk. Extraction note: "transcribed exactly as printed".
- **WTI timeframe.** Caption "Ví dụ: WTICO D1" vs chart header "West Texas Oil, 1h, OANDA" (WA p47).
- **WTI duplicate level.** đáy #3 "được kết thúc ở (79.800)" and later đỉnh #1' "chỉ kiểm tra và kết thúc ở (79.800)" (WA p47) — the same price is printed for the pullback low and the later failed-test high.
- **List item 4 wording.** Body list: "Bắt đầu thay đổi xu hướng" (WA p61) vs diagram label "4. Khởi phát xu hướng" [không chắc] (WA p61). The extraction records: "cả hai đều được ghi lại trung thực theo đúng vị trí xuất hiện trên trang".
- **Five items vs four stages.** The p59 diagram is split into **four** regions (xu hướng / dừng / đi ngang / chuyển tiếp) while the p61 diagram of the same data is split into **five** regions (Dừng … Xác nhận), immediately after p60 states "4 giai đoạn trên chính là sự phát triển của các sự kiện từ Phase A đến Phase E" (five phases). The mapping 4-stages → 5-items → 5-phases is asserted, not drawn.
- **BTC/USDT p56 chart header vs axis.** Header OHLC "approx O24851, H24850, L24824, C24824" while the visible price axis is "~16600.0 to ~21600.0" (WA p56) — the header quote lies outside the plotted axis; the extraction marks the header approximate/obscured.
- **Dow quotation rendered two ways.** "Tất cả thông tin đều phản ánh qua giá và khối lượng." (WA p4) vs "Mọi thông tin đều được phản ánh vào giá và khối lượng" (WA p65).
- **Cross-file:** Dalton's book title "Mind Over the Market" (WA p50) vs "Mind Over Markets" in `knowledge/08` (WMT p232) — see BLOCK E.

### 9.3 PHẦN 3

**`[không chắc: …]` flags in pp. 184–220:** none. The extraction parts record no `[không chắc: …]` flag on any of these pages (part-05 photos 124–134; part-06 photos 135–142). The part-05 batch note states "Unreadable / [không đọc được] photos: none."

**Legibility / unreadable chart labels:**
- WA p194 — AUD/USD continuation chart: the triangle/wedge overlay's "labelled points not individually legible at this resolution — matches the A/B/D triangle schematic from page 192/193" (part-05, Photo 129). No levels can be cited for AUD/USD.
- WA p197 — instrument transcribed as "BSC/USDT (or similar futures pair per small header text)" — the pair name is uncertain; cited here as printed in the part.
- WA p196 — instrument header transcribed as "MATIC/Tether (per chart header)".
- WA p183 (outside range, for context) and p213 show bleed-through of the PHẦN 3 / PHẦN 4 dividers; no content lost.

**Internal inconsistencies between pages (both sides quoted; not resolved):**
1. *Accumulation volume vs price wording.* WA p185: "khối lượng tích lũy sẽ có xu hướng giảm dần từ Phase C trở về sau" vs WA p186: "khối lượng của tích lũy không lớn và cứ đều đều dàn trải và **giá** có xu hướng giảm dần từ Phase E trở đi" — the p186 sentence says *price* declines from Phase E in an accumulation; recorded verbatim.
2. *"ba dấu hiệu" vs two hình thái.* WA p191: "có ba dấu hiệu về khối lượng, đây là hình thái khối lượng. Thứ nhất là …" vs WA p194 Tóm tắt enumerating only "Hình thái khối lượng thứ 1" and "Hình thái khối lượng thứ 2". A third is never printed in pp. 185–194.
3. *p190 diagram vs p191 text for hình thái 1.* Part-05 describes the p190 volume schematic as "bars under A tallest, decreasing under B, then a small cluster under C, and a taller cluster of bars under D" while WA p191 says "Thứ nhất là khối lượng tăng dần như hình minh họa phía trên … Phase C và Phase D, khối lượng tăng dần cao nhất". As transcribed, the diagram's A-tallest/B-lower shape resembles hình thái 2 rather than the "tăng dần" hình thái 1 it is said to illustrate.
4. *CO selling at SOS.* WA p201 ledger: "Tại SOS … CO sẽ tiếp tục bán ra 200.000 sản phẩm để kiểm tra lại nguồn cung tiềm ẩn … tạo thành BU/LPS" vs WA p202 boxed Quan trọng: "Sự khác biệt lớn sau SOS, CO không bán bất kỳ tài sản nào của họ" and WA p203: "giá sẽ không giảm xuống mức hỗ trợ nếu CO không có chủ đích bán hàng của họ".
5. *Diagram vs text after BU/LPS (accumulation).* WA p199 diagram: circled "BUC (800k)" with "+100k" near BUC to 1000k at the final peak, vs WA p201 text: "tạo thành BU/LPS … còn lại 800.000 … CO gom thêm được 200.000 sản phẩm nữa và tổng … 1 triệu". The diagram label is "BUC", the text says "BU/LPS"; the diagram's +100k does not reach 1,000k from 800k, the text's +200.000 does.
6. *Distribution diagram vs text — event order and the UT step.* WA p204 diagram order and totals: "BC −500k=500k → ST −200k=400k → AR +100k=600k → UT −100k=Flat → SOW +100k=500k → UTAD −300k=200k" vs WA p205–206 text order: BCLX −500.000 (500.000) → AR +100.000 (600.000) → ST[A] −200.000 (400.000) → mSOW +100.000 (500.000) → "bán tiếp 300.000". The diagram places ST before AR (and its running total 400k→600k does not add from +100k), prints a "UT −100k=Flat" step that has no counterpart in the text, and labels the dip "SOW" where the text says "mSOW".
7. *Sub-label on the accumulation schematic.* WA p199 (accumulation ledger) carries the sub-label "Hành động CO ở TR (Re/Distribution)" — the same label as the distribution schematic on WA p204 — although the p199 diagram and text are the tích lũy / tái tích lũy case.
8. *BCLX on the Urgent-Demand accumulation chart.* WA p197 chart labels its early consolidation "BCLX, ST" with an "AR" box beneath, on a structure that then prints SPRING → SOS → BU/LPS (an accumulation). Recorded as printed.
9. *Annotation "CO gây gay mua vào".* WA p197 chart annotation as transcribed; printed wording kept verbatim.
10. *"1 áo mua".* WA p211: "Năm khách lẻ muốn mua 1 áo mua" — transcribed as printed.
11. *Producer "lợi nhuận" 16$.* WA p211 computes 7$+4$+5$ = 16$ as "Tổng cộng", WA p214 calls the same 16$ "lợi nhuận" for the producer and 22$ "lợi nhuận" for CO (whose 22$ is revenue 29$ minus cost 7$). Recorded as printed.

**Page-boundary gap vs the printed TOC:**
- The TOC (WA p6–7) gives PHẦN 3 §5 Case study = p208 and PHẦN 4 TAPE READING = p221 (§1 Tape reading Bar by Bar = p221). The parts show the case study running p208–214, the **PHẦN 4 divider at p215**, Tape-Reading front matter on pp. 216–220, and "1. Tape reading Bar by Bar" at p221. Consequently pp. 216–220 (history, BẢNG MÔ TẢ KÝ HIỆU, chênh lệch / phạm vi thực, "khối lượng lớn = phe đối lập") are PHẦN 4 material printed inside this fragment's page range. They are covered compactly in §3.6 / WA3-25–26 / P3-13; the stitcher should keep one copy only if the PHẦN 4 fragment also covers them.
- WA p205→p206: the sentence "thì lại một lần nữa CO" (p205) continues as "bán tiếp 300.000 sản phẩm" (p206) across the part-05/part-06 boundary; both halves are quoted in §3.4.3.
- No page in 185–220 is missing from the parts; part-05 covers 184–205 and part-06 covers 206–249 continuously.

**Things the book names but does not define in these pages:** PSY (listed among the spike events, WA p186, p194 — defined in the glossary WA p8); "BUC" (diagram-only label, WA p199); "Flat" (diagram-only running total, WA p204); no stop-loss placement rule is printed anywhere in pp. 185–220.

### 9.4 PHẦN 4

**`[không chắc]` flags carried over verbatim from the parts (pp. 221–293):**
1. WA p290 (photo 177 diagram): `[không chắc: exact wording/placement of the four annotation labels on this diagram — labels are legible but their precise attachment to which swing-leg pair is partly ambiguous from the photo; core concept (comparing consecutive swing-leg distances to call strength/weakness) is clear.]`
2. WA p291 (photo 178 diagram): `[không chắc: exact label-to-leg mapping, as in photo 177's diagram]`

No other `[không chắc: …]` flags occur in the part entries for pp. 221–293.

**Extraction notes that are not `[không chắc]` flags but affect fidelity:**
- WA p227: instrument printed as "USD/UCAD" — part-06 note: "likely a typo for USD/CAD; transcribed verbatim as printed." Kept as printed above.
- WA p293: a mirrored heading "GIỚI THIỆU TÁC GIẢ VÀ CỘNG SỰ" is bleed-through from p294, not p293 text.

**Internal inconsistencies noticed between pages (both sides quoted; not resolved):**
- **Nến 8 of example 1 (WA p239):** prose says "Nỗ lực giảm giá nến 8 **nhỏ hơn** nỗ lực giảm giá nến 6" but the bracket reads "(dE8**>**dE6, dR8>dR6) => Giảm". Effort direction in prose vs notation disagree.
- **Profile-type count:** WA p265 "chúng ta có thể phân thành **3 loại** Volume Profile" (then names Fixed range and Session Profile, plus Fixed/Variable modes on p269 and a "Volume Profile dạng tổng hợp" on p269) vs the summary box WA p276 "Có **2 loại** cấu hình Volume Profile: Fixed Range, Session Profile". The third type is never named in the transcribed text.
- **XAU/USD M5 annotation boxes (WA p252):** recorded as "5.801(0.36%) 1988.1" and "6.505(0.27%) 450.5" — a larger point move (6.505) paired with a smaller percentage (0.27%) than 5.801/0.36% is numerically inconsistent on a ~1640 price base; the body text's 0.36% > 0.27% is what the reading uses. Cannot tell whether the chart box or the transcription is off.
- **Downtrend Case 4 examples (WA p235–236):** the p235 chart's faint header reads "US Dollar/Canadian Dollar **M5**" while the p236 example heading reads "Ví dụ: USD/CAD **M15**".
- **Downtrend Case 3 (WA p233–234):** the same USD/CAD M15 sentence is printed twice with two different charts ("khối lượng đang giảm dần, biên độ nến giảm. Nỗ lực giảm kết quả cũng giảm kịch bản đảo chiều tăng giá" on p233; near-identical on p234).
- **Phase-volume sentence (WA p249):** "Phase C có khối lượng giảm, khối lượng sẽ giảm từ A đến B sau đó lại tăng từ B đến C." The first clause (Phase C volume falls) and the second (volume rises from B to C) read against each other; recorded as printed.
- **Up-wave figures (WA p251):** "Sóng đầu với khối lượng tương đối, tăng được (17%) so với khối lượng của sóng 2 (18.69%). Vì khối lượng không tăng … đánh giá khu vực 18% lượng cầu giảm." It is not stated whether 17% / 18.69% / 16% are volume shares or price extensions, nor what "khu vực 18%" denotes.
- **P-shape wording (WA p271, p277):** "P-shape Profile là đặc trưng của xu hướng tăng và đại diện cho mô hình **phân phối hoặc tái tích lũy**" alongside WA p272 "trong quá trình phát triển nội tại của một xu hướng tăng dài hạn … chúng chỉ đơn giản là các vùng **tích lũy**" — "tích lũy" vs "tái tích lũy" used for the same in-trend P-shape.
- **Bar-by-Bar close position:** the cases (WA p221–237) never state a close-position criterion, although Analog Bar comparisons do use "giá đóng cửa" (WA p244). The E/R table on WA p217 (outside this fragment) lists "Giá đóng cửa" as a measurement input.

**Chart labels the parts could not read (faint TradingView captions / unlabelled charts):**
- WA p221 chart "labelled (BTC/USDT-style, Binance)" — label inferred, not read.
- WA p223 (first chart), p225 (two charts), p227 (two charts), p229 (two charts), p230, p231 (first chart), p232 (two charts): instruments unlabelled; only the body text names the instrument.
- WA p233: "US Dollar/Canadian Dollar per faint label, M15"; WA p234: "USD/CAD M15 (labelled at top, faint)"; WA p235: "US Dollar/Canadian Dollar M5 per faint label"; WA p237: "Gold Root / U.S. Dollar, M30, per faint header".
- WA p240–242 (Ví dụ 2) and p243–245 (Analog Bar), p246 (context diagram), p247–249 (Swing-by-Swing chart): instrument not recorded.
- WA p263: "unnamed instrument (label partly illegible, appears to be an FX/CFD pair)".
- WA p271: "instrument label partly illegible (BTC-related, Binance, Ticks)".
- WA p279, p280, p282, p283, p285, p287: candlestick charts unlabelled; p283 and p285 show a "PERPETUAL FUTURES, 1S, Binance"-style label without an instrument name.

**Range-boundary / stitching flags for the main session:**
- The PHẦN 4 divider is **WA p215** and the section's opener runs **pp. 216–220** (ticker-tape history 1867; the boxed `BẢNG MÔ TẢ KÝ HIỆU` defining E, E>, E<, E>>, uE, dE, R, R>, R<, uR, dR, and the `KHỐI LƯỢNG >>> = D >>> + S>` / `= S >>> + D>` spike lines; `Định nghĩa về phạm vi thực và sự chênh lệch`; `BIẾN ĐỘNG GIÁ TRONG NGÀY`; "xuất hiện KHỐI LƯỢNG LỚN chính là dấu hiệu của việc xuất hiện phe đối lập" on p220). The dispatch assigned pp. ≤220 to the PHẦN 3 fragment; the printed TOC places PHẦN 4 §1 at p221. Whichever fragment carries pp. 215–220, the notation table on **p217** is the definition source for the `dE/dR/uE/uR` symbols used throughout §4.1–4.4 above — it should sit immediately before §4.1 in the stitched file.
- **p294** ("GIỚI THIỆU TÁC GIẢ VÀ CỘNG SỰ") was not photographed; the book's main body ends at p293. The pre-merge revision of this file listed PHẦN 4 and LỜI KẾT as "❌ not covered"; §1.1 and §1.2 above were rewritten at stitch time and now record them as covered.
- The existing §1.1 says PHẦN 4 spans "pp. 221–292" per the TOC; the extraction shows §6 SOT's closing bullets on p292 and LỜI KẾT on p293, consistent with that.

Load-bearing caveat: PS is NOT a reversal signal and does not show that price has stopped falling - it only opens the scenario that the decline may stop later.", "volume_rule": "Volume at PS is fairly large.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 72},

A climactic sell-off where demand absorbs price upward: CO makes a first test buy that creates strong upward pressure, drags in FOMO buyers and forces earlier shorts to cover, snapping price up to the upper border. The SC low (with ST) sets the TR lower border. In the worked cases SC is also 'diem so hai #1', the first fear point where retail abandon longs and create a large liquidity pool for CO.", "volume_rule": "Volume at SC is normally very large, but an SC need NOT have high volume: low volume means either supply was absorbed all the way down (low supply cannot meet large demand) or holders refuse to sell at a loss and remain latent supply. Either way expect a LONGER accumulation - the more exhausted supply is, the longer accumulation runs.", "confirmation": "The Automatic Rally (AR) that follows, lifting price to the TR upper border.", "invalidation": null, "page": 73},
```json
        {"event": "AR", "label": "AR[A]", "definition": "Automatic Rally - luc day tu dong. Occurs because heavy selling pressure has fallen away sharply; a wave of buyers (mostly retail, plus shorts taking profit) easily pushes price up. The high made by this thrust defines the upper border of the accumulation TR.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 73},
        {"event": "ST", "label": "ST[A]", "definition": "Secondary Test - thu nghiem thu cap. Checks what supply and demand are doing at that moment. The most common case is ST[A] higher than SC, and there are usually several STs across the whole structure. Its position also grades the potential and strength of the accumulation zone.", "volume_rule": "Spread narrows and volume falls compared with SC.", "confirmation": "ST above SC, and above 50% of the TR, shows supply has thinned and the TR is likely to work in its upper half.", "invalidation": "ST that drops below SC shows supply is still abundant - expect new lows or a prolonged consolidation with many further STs.", "page": 73}
      ],
      "B": [
        {"event": "UA", "label": "UA[B]", "definition": "Upthrust Action - the first action of Phase B is the break above AR. AR becomes resistance and SC becomes support; the break clears the stops that retail shorts left above AR, so CO can lift price cheaply. It is a bull trap ('Bay tang gia') that excites retail into buying, into which CO sells. CO sells at the upper border while still accumulating at the lower border - the 'kill Long kill Short' behaviour of Phase B.", "volume_rule": "The trap prints good widening-spread up candles with large volume, but price is not actually ready to advance yet.", "confirmation": "Price returns to ST[B] near the SC support after the UA; the UA high becomes new resistance.", "invalidation": null, "page": 78},
        {"event": "ST[B]", "label": "ST[B]", "definition": "The Phase B return to SC support after the UA - a test of the lower border inside the cause-building range.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 88},
        {"event": "ST[B] as mSOW", "label": "ST[B] as mSOW", "definition": "A Phase B test that BREAKS SC support - a bear trap. Pushing price through the SC support shakes out the remaining supply and also shakes out the traders who bought early at SC. In the worked accumulation this is 'diem so hai #2', the second fear point.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "A following LPS[C] on progressively lower volume shows the test at the broken low succeeded.", "invalidation": null, "page": 91}
      ],
      "C": [
        {"event": "Spring", "label": "Spring[C]", "definition": "A price move below the TR support established in Phases A and B that quickly reverses and moves back into the TR, closing inside it. It is a bear trap, because the drop below support appears to signal continuation of the downtrend; it lets strong hands make the decisive test of remaining supply and buy more cheaply before the mark-up campaign. Spring/Shakeout usually occurs late in the TR. Phase C is the most-watched stage of the accumulation model.", "volume_rule": "A LOW-volume Spring indicates the instrument is likely ready to rise - a good moment to open a partial position.", "confirmation": "The Test immediately after (supply check inside the TR), and price at least reaching the upper border of the TR; if price does not return to test SC support and rises on red candles with long lower wicks, CO is absorbing and the LPS points on the way up are buy locations.", "invalidation": "If price lingers outside the TR and most candles close below the border, it is a Shakeout, not a Spring - supply remains.", "page": 80},
        {"event": "Shakeout", "label": "Shakeout[C]", "definition": "CO deliberately makes retail believe price is going down: MOST of the Shakeout candles close BELOW the TR lower border, so holders give up thinking it is a SOW, and CO gathers enough to mark price up. Because supply still remains, price can persist out there and build its own value area.", "volume_rule": "In the worked BNB/USDT M30 case the Shakeout[C] break of 228.0 came with maximal volume.", "confirmation": "CO then pushes price back into the TR and finishes checking the residual supply inside it (the Test); dropping timeframe to find a local Spring[C]/LPS[C] gives an earlier entry and better R:R.", "invalidation": null, "page": 80},
        {"event": "Test", "label": "Test[C]", "definition": "The composite operators' check of supply across the WHOLE Trading Range, usually appearing immediately after a Spring or Shakeout. If supply feels exhausted, CO will consider marking price back up.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 80},
        {"event": "LPS[C]", "label": "LPS[C]", "definition": "Phase C WITHOUT a Spring: price breaks upward directly and no Spring is needed. Supply in the market is no longer sufficient to push price down, and the move typically starts from an LPS. Note: not every structure has a Spring or Shakeout; there are also cases where price never leaves the TR at all, showing supply is genuinely exhausted and an out-of-range test is unnecessary.", "volume_rule": "The declines into LPS[C] print falling volume.", "confirmation": "A strong breakout candle, or a small value area that supplies the entry signal - candles alone cannot tell whether Phase B has ended; this read needs volume and data knowledge.", "invalidation": null, "page": 82}
      ],
      "D": [
        {"event": "SOS", "label": "SOS[D]", "definition": "Sign of Strength - the strength of the whole structure, shown by price committing above the highest points of the TR and closing above the TR's price range. Watch for it when price is in the UPPER half of the TR. In Phase D demand demonstrably overwhelms supply and price moves at least to the TR upper border.", "volume_rule": "Widening spread with steadily rising volume; closing at the TR high on very large volume proves demand is abundant.", "confirmation": "Price exceeding the SOS high - at that point the uptrend is officially formed and Phase E begins.", "invalidation": null, "page": 84},
        {"event": "LPS", "label": "LPS[D]", "definition": "Last Point of Support - the pullback after the SOS is made, following growth out of a Spring or Shakeout. There are TWO kinds: one created after SOS and one created inside the Trading Range. These pullbacks are the best places to add to profitable long positions.", "volume_rule": "Supply volume falls away progressively into the LPS reactions.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 84},
        {"event": "BU", "label": "BU/LPS[D]", "definition": "Back Up - thorough absorption of the entire supply sitting on the TR wall after the SOS. Depending on how much supply is left, the BU takes the form of a single LPS or of a whole small structure. It is the final supply test before price enters the uptrend.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "The pullback from SOS to the TR upper border is absorbed strongly and price is driven back up, forming the BU/LPS; price is then held in the BU area and breaks through the SOS into Phase E.", "invalidation": null, "page": 84}
      ],
      "E": [
        {"event": "Phase E", "label": "Phase E", "definition": "Price action leaves the TR, supply is fully controlled and a clear strong advance follows. Failures such as shakeouts and the more typical downward reactions are short-lived. The full trading position is activated here; in trend, speed and wave length become extremely fast. After this stage price enters a new cycle - a fresh re-accumulation - whose new TR involves CO taking profit and buying more.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 86}
      ]
    },
    "phan_phoi": {
      "A": [
        {"event": "PSY", "label": "PSY[A]", "definition": "Preliminary Supply - nguon cung so bo. Where the composite operator begins selling after a strong advance, signalling that a change of trend may be near.", "volume_rule": "Comes with volume and widening price spread.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 102},
        {"event": "BCLX", "label": "BCLX[A]", "definition": "Buying Climax - cao trao mua. The public rushes in to buy at a moment when a great deal of good news is released, and the operators use it to unload their stock. BCLX marks the stopping of the prior uptrend and sets the TR's upper border.", "volume_rule": "A marked increase in volume with rising price spread.", "confirmation": "Usually followed by an Automatic Reaction (AR) and a Secondary Test (ST) of BCLX, typically on declining volume.", "invalidation": "The uptrend can also end WITHOUT any climactic action, instead showing exhaustion of demand with declining spread and volume - 'shortening of the thrust' on each advance - before significant supply appears.", "page": 102},
        {"event": "AR", "label": "AR[A]", "definition": "Automatic Reaction - phan ung tu dong. Fierce buying pressure has fallen away sharply after BCLX and small traders take profit by selling along; the low it makes is the LOWER border of the distribution Trading Range.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 102},
        {"event": "ST", "label": "ST[A]", "definition": "Secondary Test - price returns to the BCLX area to test the supply/demand balance at those levels. For CO to hold price inside the TR, supply must exceed demand as the next peak is confirmed.", "volume_rule": "Volume and spread DECLINE as price approaches the BCLX resistance, establishing ST[A].", "confirmation": "ST lower than BCLX means demand has been fading - this shortens how long the distribution range persists.", "invalidation": "ST higher than BCLX means demand is still aggressive.", "page": 103}
      ],
      "B": [
        {"event": "ST[B] as UT", "label": "ST[B] as UT", "definition": "ST[B] can take another form and create an Upthrust (UT), in which price moves ABOVE the BCLX resistance and quickly reverses to close below that resistance.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "After the UT, price usually goes on to test the LOWER border of the Trading Range.", "invalidation": null, "page": 103},
        {"event": "mSOW", "label": "mSOW[B]", "definition": "minor Sign of Weakness - dau hieu cua su suy yeu gia. Observable when price moves down to the lower border of the TR. Committing below the initial AR support shows that SUPPLY is taking control of price action.", "volume_rule": "Usually happens on a falling range with increasing spread and increasing volume.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 104},
        {"event": "UT", "label": "UT[B]", "definition": "Upthrust - hanh dong tang gia that bai. Price action created by CO that approaches or exceeds the upper border to excite the crowd, then FAILS to break the structure upward. Note that Phase B in distribution is normally SHORTER than in accumulation, because distribution is the unloading stage while accumulation is the gathering stage.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 105}
      ],
      "C": [
        {"event": "LPSY", "label": "LPSY[C]", "definition": "Last Point of Supply - diem khang cu cuoi cung. After testing the local resistance at the (as printed) lower border of the Trading Range, a feeble narrow-range recovery shows the market is having considerable difficulty rising. The failure to reach higher levels is due either to weak demand against significant supply, or to strong demand against still stronger supply. LPSY represents the exhaustion of demand and the operators' final sales before the decline becomes stronger. In distribution, Phase C can form as LPSY[C] instead of UTAD.", "volume_rule": "A feeble rally on a NARROW spread.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 107},
        {"event": "UTAD", "label": "UTAD[C]", "definition": "Upthrust After Distribution - bay tang gia chinh phan phoi. The mirror of the Spring: price commits at the TR resistance, quickly reverses and closes back inside the TR. This is the test of REMAINING DEMAND. It is a bull trap: the UT/UTAD lets the operator sell more stock at high prices to the retail buyers lifting price there. It is also described as a failed SOS that goes on to distribution. Institutions following the strong hands may open short positions after a UT or UTAD, where risk/reward is usually very large.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "Because strong hands create UTs repeatedly to stop out short sellers, it is usually SAFER to wait until Phase D and confirm the LPSY.", "invalidation": "UTAD is NOT a mandatory structural component of a distribution TR: schematic #1 contains a UTAD, schematic #2 does not.", "page": 107}
      ],
      "D": [
        {"event": "MSOW", "label": "MSOW[D]", "definition": "Major Sign of Weakness - the price move down below the spread of the Trading Range floor, usually happening very fast. The clear break of support (or of the supports inside the downward price action of the TR) is the evidence that supply is now clearly dominant, and the commitment below the AR support shows the change of character in price action. After a UT/UTAD the TR often remains present until the end of Phase D, and buyer optimism can reappear before the decline strengthens.", "volume_rule": "Comes with a spike in volume on the break.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 109},
        {"event": "LPSY", "label": "LPSY[D]", "definition": "Last Point of Supply restated for Phase D: after testing the local resistance at the lower border of the Trading Range, a feeble narrow-range recovery shows the market struggling badly to rise - the exhaustion of demand and the final sales of both investors and the composite operators before the decline becomes stronger. In Phase D the strongest downward price action appears.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "Price is held in the area below the TR border and then breaks on through the SOW to step into Phase E.", "invalidation": null, "page": 110}
      ],
      "E": [
        {"event": "Phase E", "label": "Phase E", "definition": "The stage where price officially declines. In this stage price action enters a cycle: price moves into a new re-distribution phase, or into a new accumulation.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 111}
      ]
    },
    "tai_tich_luy": {
      "note": "Re-accumulation is exactly the same model as accumulation; the only difference is the starting context - it develops out of an UPTREND, as a pause in which CO adds strength to the advance in progress (WA p124, restated p140). Its Phase A is identical in nature to DISTRIBUTION (PSY, BCLX, AR, ST) - both shake out the weakly-aware participants and optimise CO's observation position (WA p126-127); so do not short the BCLX on its own. Point-and-Figure can be used to mark the start and end of re-accumulation ranges, and '3CHoBEV=ChoCH' is observed at the top of the prior mark-up before looking for a small Value Arena and shifting timeframe (WA p125). Whether a range is re-accumulation or distribution is only knowable AFTER the model completes (WA p125). Four sub-types are named (boxed WA p141): (1) 'Tai tich luy voi Spring Action' (WA p126-130) - the only sub-type where CO's demand absorption runs in a shorter, very fast, aggressive window; (2) 'Tai tich luy the hien suc manh' (WA p130-133) - the STRONGEST form: higher highs, BCLX and AR form the TR with NO Spring, only LPS, then price goes straight to SOS and forms Phase D immediately, absorbing residual supply at the Creek and LPS; (3) 'Tai tich luy voi thoi gian hap thu nguon cung lau hon' (WA p134-136) - runs deeper, and a buy entry is not easy; (4) 'Tai tich luy sau khi suy giam' (WA p136-138) - the WEAKEST form, the decline starting from a small local distribution pattern with a Local SC, where absorption is often incomplete. Position note (WA p141): the higher the re-accumulation sits in the sequence (2nd, 3rd...), the more likely CO decides to switch into distribution rather than maintain the structure.",
      "A": [
        {"event": "PSY", "label": "PSY[A] (cuc bo)", "definition": "After price has risen out of accumulation the first event is a LOCAL PSY, followed by price FOMO and maximal buying demand.         {"event": "BCLX", "label": "BCLX[A]", "definition": "The buying climax of the re-accumulation Phase A, where CO and other participants take profit. Worked levels: WTICO 88.000; NEAR/USDT 13.200; BNB/USDT 114.00 (as recorded); AXS/USDT 12.00 (unclear digit).", "volume_rule": "Volume normally rises strongly in this BCLX area.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 126},
        {"event": "AR", "label": "AR[A]", "definition": "The automatic reaction that, with BCLX, sets the borders of the re-accumulation TR; Phase A here behaves exactly as in distribution.         {"event": "ST", "label": "ST[A]", "definition": "Secondary test in Phase A; tests continue at BOTH borders exactly as they do in distribution.       ],
      "B": [
        {"event": "ST[B]", "label": "ST[B]", "definition": "Phase B of re-accumulation begins with continuous supply/demand test events at the UPPER and LOWER borders of the Trading Range.       ],
      "C": [
        {"event": "Spring", "label": "Spring[C]", "definition": "In Phase C the CO decides according to how much LATENT supply is left: if little, it drives price on up to form SOS and then LPS in Phase D; if much, it keeps testing the latent supply for longer. In the Spring sub-type the Phase C event is a Spring that breaks below AR and is bought back by CO. Worked: WTICO M5 Spring[C] breaks below AR 87.200, CO re-buys around 86.750-87.200.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": "Price returns strongly into the range, completing Phase C; demand tests then hold (WTICO tests at 87.200 and 87.600).", "invalidation": null, "page": 128},
        {"event": "LPS", "label": "LPS[C]", "definition": "In the 'suc manh' sub-type there is NO Spring at all - only an LPS - and price goes on to SOS, forming Phase D immediately, because CO is in a hurry and drives price up.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 131}
      ],
      "D": [
        {"event": "SOS", "label": "SOS[D]", "definition": "When latent supply is little, CO pushes price up and forms the SOS, after which the LPS of Phase D follows.         {"event": "LPS", "label": "LPS[D]", "definition": "The Phase D pullback after SOS, where residual supply on the TR wall is absorbed at the Creek/LPS to finish Phase D.       ],
      "E": [
        {"event": "Phase E", "label": "Phase E", "definition": "The uptrend resumes out of the re-accumulation range. The key structural tell across the whole model is the volume behaviour running into this stage.", "volume_rule": "Volume falling progressively from Phase C through to Phase E is one of the MOST IMPORTANT signs for judging whether the re-accumulation structure is effective.", "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 130}
      ]
    },
    "tai_phan_phoi": {
      "note": "Re-distribution is a model completely identical to distribution; the difference is the context - what precedes it is a LONG DOWNTREND (WA p142). The purpose of CO in a re-distribution structure is to distribute at the best possible prices, taking them at UT[B], UTAD[C] and mSOW[B]; LPSY[C] and LPSY[D] are where the remaining supply is checked and where Short positions are added, and BUEC together with LPSY[C] is called the opportunity for large profit (WA p149). A no-UTAD[C] variant exists (WA p147). The printed Phase A schematic labels PS, AR, ST, SC (WA p138, p142).",
      "A": [
        {"event": "PS", "label": "PS[A]", "definition": "After the long decline the CO joins in and begins absorbing the stock of the weak hands at the PS/SC panic.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 142},
        {"event": "SC", "label": "SC[A]", "definition": "The panic low of Phase A where CO absorbs weak hands' stock before pushing price up.         {"event": "AR", "label": "AR[A]", "definition": "CO pushes price up to the AR, which forms the Trading Range together with the PS/SC low.", "volume_rule": null, "confirmation": null, "invalidation": null, "page": 143},
        {"event": "ST", "label": "ST[A]", "definition": "The Phase A secondary test of the re-distribution schematic.       ],
      "B": [
        {"event": "UT", "label": "UT[B]", "definition": "In Phase B price tests the upper border and then breaks out through the AR - this is a UT[B], which turns that level into a resistance zone and takes liquidity.         {"event": "ST[B]", "label": "ST[B] / mSOS", "definition": "CO keeps testing with ST(B) and mSOS through Phase B. mSOW[B] is named in the boxed summary as one of CO's best selling zones.         {"event": "LPSY", "label": "LPSY[C]", "definition": "In the no-UTAD variant Phase C forms as LPSY[C] instead.       ],
      "E": [

  "rules": [
    {"id": "WA2-01", "if": "three CHoBEV occur, each showing a reaction against the current trend, increased spread, and increased effort/volume on the reversal reaction", "then": "declare CHoCH: the prior trend is no longer maintained and a Trading Range may be drawn", "page": 68},
    {"id": "WA2-02", "if": "CHoCH is confirmed after a decline", "then": "draw the TR with lower border = start of the first up-wave and upper border = where price reversed after that up-wave; in tich luy the SC/ST lows and the AR high set the borders, in phan phoi the AR low is the lower border and BCLX the upper", "page": 69},
    {"id": "WA2-03", "if": "a CHoBEV or CHoCH is misidentified", "then": "the whole phase labelling is wrong and every trade decision derived from it is wrong - treat CHoBEV/CHoCH as the first, gating step", "page": 71},
    {"id": "WA2-04", "if": "you hold a profitable long and a notably large counter-trend reaction appears (the largest decline inside the uptrend)", "then": "watch for two outcomes - consolidation into a new TR, or a test of the highs followed by continued decline; do not assume the original trend continues", "page": 70},
    {"id": "WA2-05", "if": "PS prints with wide-range bars and large volume after a long decline", "then": "treat it only as the first hint of demand, NOT a reversal signal - start building the 'price will stop falling' scenario, do not act", "page": 73},
    {"id": "WA2-06", "if": "ST[A] holds above SC (the most common case)", "then": "supply is drying up; if ST[A] instead breaks below SC, expect new lows or a prolonged consolidation with many further STs", "page": 72},
    {"id": "WA2-07", "if": "ST[A] sits above 50% of the TR (AR-SC)", "then": "supply has thinned and the TR is likely to operate in its upper half; if ST[A] sits below 50%, supply remains abundant and strong fear points are expected later", "page": 75},
    {"id": "WA2-08", "if": "SC volume is low", "then": "read it as either supply absorbed all the way down, or holders refusing to sell at a loss (latent supply) - in either case expect a LONGER accumulation", "page": 76},
    {"id": "WA2-09", "if": "price breaks above AR early in Phase B", "then": "treat it as a bull trap (UA) where CO sells to clear the stops above resistance; do not buy the breakout", "page": 78},
    {"id": "WA2-10", "if": "Phase B shows balanced tests at both borders with no unusual volume and no sign of CO", "then": "do not trade - wait for Phase C", "page": 95},
    {"id": "WA2-11", "if": "price breaks below TR support and quickly reverses to close back inside the TR on LOW volume (Spring)", "then": "this is a high-probability moment to open a partial long; confirmation is the Test immediately after and price at least reaching the TR upper border", "page": 80},
    {"id": "WA2-12", "if": "most candles of the break close BELOW the lower border and price lingers there forming its own value area", "then": "label Shakeout (supply remains), not Spring; expect a longer test and prefer dropping timeframe to find a local Spring[C]/LPS[C]", "page": 80},
    {"id": "WA2-13", "if": "no Spring/Shakeout appears and price turns up from inside the TR (LPS[C])", "then": "wait for a strong breakout candle or a small value area to provide the entry signal - candles alone cannot tell whether Phase B has ended", "page": 82},
    {"id": "WA2-14", "if": "after a Spring price does NOT return to test SC support and rises with red candles showing long lower wicks", "then": "CO is absorbing residual supply - the LPS points on the way up are buy locations with excellent R:R", "page": 89},
    {"id": "WA2-15", "if": "price commits above the TR high with widening spread and steadily rising volume (SOS)", "then": "Phase D is in force - add to longs at LPS[D] pullbacks; and if price then holds briefly on the top of the TR wall (BU as a single LPS or a small structure), the last supply test is complete", "page": 84},
    {"id": "WA2-16", "if": "price exceeds the SOS high", "then": "conclude the uptrend is officially formed - Phase E begins", "page": 85},
    {"id": "WA2-17", "if": "a structure is only being labelled at the SOS stage", "then": "you are late - the value of Wyckoff labelling lies in Phase C", "page": 77},
    {"id": "WA2-18", "if": "you are at a potential Shakeout[C]/UTAD on the higher timeframe", "then": "drop to a lower timeframe: a local accumulation/distribution TR whose own Spring[C]/LPS[C] (or LPSY) agrees with the HTF context gives an earlier entry, a tighter stop and better R:R", "page": 93},
    {"id": "WA2-19", "if": "you want to decide whether to hold or exit", "then": "move UP a timeframe to see where the current TR sits inside the larger TR, and use the larger TR's AR/SOS as the target", "page": 98},
    {"id": "WA2-20", "if": "the higher-timeframe range shows a prior mSOW (strong supply) while the lower-timeframe range looks bullish at ST[B]", "then": "re-examine holding the long; the sell candidate is the point that is simultaneously LPSY of the larger TR, UTAD of the smaller TR and mSOW of the larger TR", "page": 121},
    {"id": "WA2-21", "if": "ST[A] in distribution is above BCLX", "then": "demand is still aggressive; if it is below BCLX, demand is fading - this sets how long the distribution range persists", "page": 103},
    {"id": "WA2-22", "if": "ST[B] pushes above BCLX resistance and quickly closes back below it (UT)", "then": "expect the next move to test the TR's lower border", "page": 103},
    {"id": "WA2-23", "if": "price commits below the AR support in Phase B with increasing spread and volume (mSOW)", "then": "supply is dominant", "page": 104},
    {"id": "WA2-24", "if": "a UT/UTAD prints", "then": "a short has very large R:R, BUT because strong hands use UTs repeatedly to stop shorts it is usually SAFER to wait for Phase D and the LPSY confirmation", "page": 106},
    {"id": "WA2-25", "if": "the rally after a supply test is weak and narrow-spread (LPSY)", "then": "demand is exhausted and CO is making its final sales - short at LPSY[C]/LPSY[D]", "page": 107},
    {"id": "WA2-26", "if": "price breaks the TR floor quickly with a volume spike (MSOW[D]) and the rally back is negligible", "then": "the distribution is completing - take further shorts at the LPSY[D] retests", "page": 109},
    {"id": "WA2-27", "if": "a lower-border break occurs in a distribution candidate", "then": "prepare BOTH scenarios - Spring (it is really accumulation) and MSOW (it is distribution) - and let the reaction decide", "page": 115},
    {"id": "WA2-28", "if": "the structure is a re-accumulation", "then": "expect Phase A to look exactly like distribution (PSY, BCLX, AR, ST); do not short the BCLX on its own", "page": 126},
    {"id": "WA2-29", "if": "volume falls progressively from Phase C to Phase E inside an uptrend pause", "then": "the re-accumulation is effective - one of the most important tells", "page": 130},
    {"id": "WA2-30", "if": "a re-accumulation shows higher highs with only LPS (no Spring) and jumps to SOS immediately", "then": "it is the 'suc manh' sub-type, the strongest - enter on LPS/Creek retests", "page": 130},
    {"id": "WA2-31", "if": "a re-accumulation starts from a small local distribution (Local SC)", "then": "it is the weakest sub-type ('sau khi suy giam') - absorption is often incomplete, so do not force a buy", "page": 136},
    {"id": "WA2-32", "if": "a re-accumulation is the 2nd, 3rd or later one in the trend", "then": "the higher it sits the more likely CO switches to distribution - raise the bar for confirmation", "page": 141},
    {"id": "WA2-33", "if": "a Phase B test in a re-distribution pushes above AR (UT[B]), or forms UTAD[C] or mSOW[B]", "then": "those are CO's best selling zones; LPSY[C]/LPSY[D] are the short entries that verify remaining supply", "page": 143},
    {"id": "WA2-34", "if": "ST[A] lies in the upper 1/3 of the TR", "then": "support is very strong and demand strong - an early sign of accumulation; if ST[A] lies in the lower 1/3 or breaks the local SC support, selling power is strong - an early sign of distribution", "page": 150},
    {"id": "WA2-35", "if": "Phase B repeatedly tests the TOP of the structure (UA) and cannot reach the bottom", "then": "expect an upward breakout from a mid-structure LPS; if Phase B repeatedly tests the BOTTOM, expect weakness and a potential LPSY as the Phase C test", "page": 154},
    {"id": "WA2-36", "if": "a Phase C shake is genuine", "then": "price must at least reach the opposite side of the structure, and ideally break the structure - if it cannot, the shake is rejected", "page": 153},
    {"id": "WA2-37", "if": "the shake was only 'mot LPS don thuan' (no Spring)", "then": "rely on the prior structural bias and keep updating as new information arrives", "page": 161},
    {"id": "WA2-38", "if": "Phase D breakout candles show large volume and spread but price is NOT sustained and demand effort then fades", "then": "it is a rejection back into the structure - open a short ONLY when aggressive selling negates the earlier weak demand", "page": 163},
    {"id": "WA2-39", "if": "you are tempted to label mechanically at UT[B]", "then": "first read effort vs result there - UT[B] can also be a potential SOS, so short only on divergence plus rejection; likewise MSOW[B] can be a potential Spring, so long only on exhaustion/absorption plus rejection at the lower border", "page": 166},
    {"id": "WA2-40", "if": "the cause and the result are already fully developed", "then": "there is no early trade left - you must enter before the result is complete, using effort-result to time the imbalance", "page": 167},
    {"id": "WA2-41", "if": "a structure slopes", "then": "rotate it mentally to horizontal - all events and phases still apply; only the controlling side into the Phase C test differs", "page": 180},
    {"id": "WA2-42", "if": "you are a first-time Wyckoff operator", "then": "use only horizontal schematics; the author explicitly does NOT encourage trading sloped patterns because the slope has no standard and the TR becomes subjective", "page": 167},
    {"id": "WA2-43", "if": "a rising-slope accumulation is in progress", "then": "wait for the BU area where absorption commits long enough to show demand dominance", "page": 170},
    {"id": "WA2-44", "if": "a falling-slope accumulation shows successive lows travelling shorter distances (SOT)", "then": "expect a failure to maintain the structure and a final supply/demand test before an upturn", "page": 172},
    {"id": "WA2-45", "if": "a falling-slope distribution forms", "then": "the Phase C test is the key to the downward break - the bigger the shock above the upper border the stronger the reversal impulse; price will move very fast, hard to enter, but at least do not buy", "page": 174},
    {"id": "WA2-46", "if": "price is still rising in what looks like a rising-slope distribution", "then": "in Phase B it is only an assumption - wait for the Phase C action (UTAD) before treating it as distribution", "page": 177},
    {"id": "WA2-47", "if": "price bounces BEFORE reaching TR support", "then": "demand is overpowering supply - expect an upward exit; if price turns down BEFORE reaching resistance, supply is overpowering - expect a downward exit", "page": 182},
    {"id": "WA2-48", "if": "you need the only explicit stop placement in these pages", "then": "BNB/USDT M5 long at 226.0 with stop at 220.0 just below the Shakeout/Spring low and target at the M30 SOS extreme 248.0, giving R:R 1:4; elsewhere the book states entries (Spring[C], LPS[C], LPS[D], ST[B], UTAD[C], LPSY[C], LPSY[D]) and targets (opposite border, HTF AR or SOS) but gives no further numeric stop rule", "page": 96}
  ]
}
```

```json
    {"n": 2, "color": "Mau xanh lam", "name": "Change of Character Analog Bars", "gloss": "thanh nen thay doi tinh cach", "worked_comparison": "range-1 effort >= range-2, range-2 result weaker => supports bullish range", "page": 243},
    {"n": 3, "color": "Mau xanh duong", "name": "Selling Climax Analog Bars", "gloss": "Thanh nen ban cuc dai", "worked_comparison": "range-2 climax = two candles merged; effort >= range-1 but spread and close weaker => bullish range", "page": 243},
    {"n": 4, "color": "Mau vang", "name": "Test Of Climactic Action Analog Bars", "gloss": "Thanh nen kiem tra hanh dong cua su cao trao truoc do", "worked_comparison": "range-2 test effort >= range-1 but spread smaller => divergence, bullish range", "page": 243},
    {"n": 5, "color": "Mau tim", "name": "Up Effort off The Test Analog Bars", "gloss": "No luc tang de kiem tra", "worked_comparison": "range-2 supply test succeeded, closes higher than range-1 with large demand tail", "page": 244}

  "swing_rules": [
    {"id": "SW-1", "rule": "Fix context first (trend vs trading range); then score price extension %, volume, structure, Wyckoff context", "page": 247},
    {"id": "SW-2", "rule": "Compare the two primary down-swings from range resistance, then the smaller test down-swings behind them", "page": 248},
    {"id": "SW-3", "rule": "Compare effort by the average volume of the whole swing; lower average on swing 2 = supply decreasing, not yet accumulation", "page": 248},
    {"id": "SW-4", "rule": "Structure: spread expansion present in decline 1 but absent in decline 2 leans bullish; use only for potential-trend inference after the range completes", "page": 249},
    {"id": "SW-5", "rule": "Secondary down-swing with more effort but fast return into range = potential Phase C low; then small range = potential Phase D with LPS", "page": 249},
    {"id": "SW-6", "rule": "For up-swings the key comparison is the volume at the start of each wave", "page": 251},
    {"id": "SW-7", "rule": "dE#1>dE#2 and dR#1>dR#2 on down-swings, uE#1<uE#2 and uR#1<uR#2 on up-swings = potential accumulation range", "page": 252},
    {"id": "SW-8", "rule": "After decline 2 stalls into a small Trading Range, enter on a Bar-by-Bar break with large effort and large result in harmony", "page": 254},
    {"id": "SW-9", "rule": "Larger decline 2 that commits below the TR low but re-enters immediately = demand present; with stronger up-swing 2 = accumulation TR", "page": 254}
  ],

    {"instrument": "AUD/USD", "timeframe": "M5", "down_swing_1_pct": 0.34, "down_swing_2_pct": 0.43, "up_swing_1_pct": 0.40, "up_swing_2_pct": 0.49, "conclusion": "Trading Range tich luy", "page": 254}
  ],
  "volume_profile": {
    "history": {"designer": "J. Peter Steidlmayer", "year": 1985, "for": "CBOT", "page": 255},
    "components": [
      {"name": "Value Area (VA), VAH, VAL", "definition": "Between VAH and VAL; part of the first standard deviation; exactly 68.2% of the profile's volume; accepted zone; VAH/VAL act as support/resistance; outside = 31.8%, rejected", "page": 259},
      {"name": "Extremes (Cao/Thap)", "definition": "Highest and lowest price of the profile; main reference points; represent completed or uncompleted auctions", "page": 260},
      {"name": "Dau gia hoan thanh (completed auction)", "definition": "Volume tapering toward the extreme; clear rejection; a low-volume zone", "page": 260},
      {"name": "Dau gia chua hoan thanh (uncompleted auction)", "definition": "High-volume zone at the end of the profile; price expected to return; in Market Profile = Poor High/Poor Low with at least 2 TPO; completed = Single Print", "page": 261},
      {"name": "VPOC", "definition": "Highest volume concentration in the profile; fairest price; level from which the VA is computed; above = buyers control (Long), below = sellers control (Short); avoid its vicinity; always an HVN but not every HVN is a VPOC", "page": 262},
      {"name": "HVN", "definition": "Balance and high interest of all participants; past balance acts as a magnet; used for targets; several per profile", "page": 264},
      {"name": "LVN", "definition": "Imbalance/rejection; valleys in the profile; interesting support/resistance for potential entries; Wyckoff structure limits are always LVN", "page": 264},
      {"name": "Fixed range", "definition": "Manually drawn over any specific price action; useful in trend and in trading range; not updated", "page": 265},
      {"name": "Session Profile", "definition": "Daily profile from session start to end, updated intraday; modes Fixed (choose day range) and Variable (all prices visible on chart, changes when chart moves)", "page": 267},
      {"name": "Volume at time vs Volume at price", "definition": "Vertical classic volume says when large traders acted; profile says where", "page": 270},
      {"name": "P-shape Profile", "definition": "Characteristic of uptrend; distribution or reaccumulation; imbalance first then rotation; after long downtrend warns of its end; inverted P = distribution", "page": 271},
      {"name": "B-shape Profile", "definition": "Characteristic of downtrend; accumulation or redistribution; after long uptrend may signal its end", "page": 273},
      {"name": "D-shape Profile", "definition": "Most common; temporary balance; institutions building positions before a break", "page": 274}
    ],
    "rules": [
      {"id": "VP-1", "rule": "When price imbalances, look first at the nearest previous balance zone; older zones matter less", "page": 257},
      {"id": "VP-2", "rule": "Require a completed auction at the lower part of a balance before expecting an upside exit; an uncompleted auction there cancels the scenario until tested", "page": 261},
      {"id": "VP-3", "rule": "When in doubt, treat an auction as completed", "page": 261},
      {"id": "VP-4", "rule": "Above VPOC prefer Long, below VPOC prefer Short; avoid trading near VPOC", "page": 263},
      {"id": "VP-5", "rule": "HVN for targets; LVN for entries", "page": 264},
      {"id": "VP-6", "rule": "Fixed Range over a trend to find the VPOC test; over a Wyckoff structure to find VPOC and VAH/VAL for the post-break test; one Fixed Range over overlapping session VAs", "page": 266},
      {"id": "VP-7", "rule": "When an uptrend pauses, look for a combination (pinbar, double bottom/top) at VPOC; first continuation test above prior session VA is the prior VAH", "page": 268},
      {"id": "VP-8", "rule": "Mark LVN/HVN above and below price for macro bias; long-term HVN for take-profit; big LVN + Shakeout = potential accumulation; LVN near structure = possible Spring", "page": 269},
      {"id": "VP-9", "rule": "Use VAH/VAL to define unclear structures; structure limits are LVN", "page": 275},
      {"id": "VP-10", "rule": "Trade with the last HVN created; above HVN = buyers control, sell only after price crosses it downward; no direction call inside an HVN until the break is confirmed", "page": 275}
    ]
  },
  "sot": {
    "definition": "SOT la mot loai mo hinh dao chieu the hien mat dong luong cua mot xu huong hoac mot song; diem dung moi (dinh/day) co bien do ngan hon diem dung cu; rut ngan luc day; acronym not expanded in the book",
    "page": 278,
    "min_pushes": 3,
    "useful_pushes": "3-4",
    "too_strong_pushes": "more than 4",
    "forms": ["phan ky (momentum down, volume large)", "kiet suc (momentum down, volume down)"],
    "candle_form": "three consecutive candles with shrinking range at an important reference",
    "worked_examples": [
      {"instrument": "BTC/USDT Futures", "timeframe": "M5", "levels": [15950, 15900, 15850], "direction": "downtrend low", "page": 279},
      {"instrument": "Gold futures (COMEX)", "timeframe": "M5", "levels": [1777, 1778, 1779], "direction": "uptrend high", "page": 279},
      {"instrument": "unlabelled", "timeframe": "not recorded", "note": "SOT at UTAD with rising volume; drop to lower TR boundary", "page": 282},
      {"instrument": "BTC/USDT Futures", "timeframe": "M5", "note": "SOT mid-range after potential UTAD failed; UTAD prevailed", "page": 285},
      {"instrument": "Gold futures", "timeframe": "H1", "note": "SOT in candle form at TR upper boundary", "page": 287}
    ]
  },
  "rules": [
    {"id": "WA4-01", "if": "uptrend context, spread TANG, volume TANG DEU", "then": "harmonious, TANG GIA continuation", "page": 221},
    {"id": "WA4-02", "if": "uptrend context, spread GIAM, volume TANG DEU", "then": "supply present, GIAM GIA", "page": 222},
    {"id": "WA4-03", "if": "uptrend context, spread GIAM, volume GIAM", "then": "demand fading, GIAM GIA", "page": 224},
    {"id": "WA4-04", "if": "uptrend context, spread TANG, volume GIAM", "then": "TANG GIA but vulnerable (de bi ton thuong)", "page": 226},
    {"id": "WA4-05", "if": "uptrend context, spread TANG, volume TANG DOT BIEN", "then": "opposing side / climax / stopping action, GIAM GIA, expect a range", "page": 228},
    {"id": "WA4-06", "if": "downtrend context, spread TANG, volume TANG DEU", "then": "harmonious, GIAM GIA continuation", "page": 230},
    {"id": "WA4-07", "if": "downtrend context, spread GIAM, volume TANG DEU", "then": "demand present, TANG GIA", "page": 231},
    {"id": "WA4-08", "if": "downtrend context, spread GIAM, volume GIAM", "then": "supply fading, TANG GIA", "page": 232},
    {"id": "WA4-09", "if": "downtrend context, spread TANG, volume GIAM DAN", "then": "GIAM GIA but vulnerable", "page": 234},
    {"id": "WA4-10", "if": "downtrend context, spread TANG, volume TANG DOT BIEN", "then": "opposing side / climax / stopping action, TANG GIA, expect a range", "page": 236},
    {"id": "WA4-11", "if": "applying Bar by Bar", "then": "read only the last bar to confirm a system signal or at important S/R; look for harmony vs divergence", "page": 243},
    {"id": "WA4-12", "if": "Truong hop 4 in either context", "then": "low confidence; a modest opposing force can reverse it", "page": 243},
    {"id": "WA4-13", "if": "reading candles sequentially", "then": "compare each bar's effort and result with the latest same-direction bar (dEn vs prior dE, uEn vs prior uE)", "page": 238},
    {"id": "WA4-14", "if": "an up-bar's effort and result exceed all prior up-bars in the range", "then": "double bottom confirmed complete, Tang", "page": 240},
    {"id": "WA4-15", "if": "a down-bar's effort and result are the largest in the range and negate prior bars", "then": "large supply appeared, kich ban giam gia", "page": 242},
    {"id": "WA4-16", "if": "a bar breaks important resistance on large volume (uE>, uR>) but following up-bars show uE<, uR<", "then": "bullish scenario, but chua the tang until effort/result exceed the breakout bar", "page": 241},
    {"id": "WA4-17", "if": "comparing analog bars", "then": "pair same-role bars across range 1 and range 2; a divergence sets the structure bias", "page": 243},
    {"id": "WA4-18", "if": "range-2 CHoCH / Selling Climax / Test bars have effort >= range-1 but weaker spread and close", "then": "supports a bullish range", "page": 244},
    {"id": "WA4-19", "if": "range-2 Up-Effort-off-the-Test closes higher than range-1 with a large demand tail", "then": "supply test succeeded; counts toward potential accumulation", "page": 245},
    {"id": "WA4-20", "if": "all five analog comparisons favour buyers", "then": "potential accumulation range", "page": 245},
    {"id": "WA4-21", "if": "uptrend then sign of supply, CHoBEV bar, climactic bar, test of the climax low", "then": "range boundaries set; exit and wait for the range to develop", "page": 246},
    {"id": "WA4-22", "if": "comparing swings", "then": "fix context, then score price extension %, volume, structure, Wyckoff context", "page": 247},
    {"id": "WA4-23", "if": "comparing down-swings", "then": "compare the two primary down-swings from resistance, then the test down-swings behind them", "page": 248},
    {"id": "WA4-24", "if": "second decline extends less and fails to reach range support", "then": "selling force decreasing, higher low, bullish range", "page": 248},
    {"id": "WA4-25", "if": "comparing swing effort", "then": "use average volume of the whole swing; lower on swing 2 = supply decreasing, not proof of accumulation", "page": 248},
    {"id": "WA4-26", "if": "decline 2 lacks the spread expansion of decline 1", "then": "structure leans bullish; use only for post-range inference", "page": 249},
    {"id": "WA4-27", "if": "secondary down-swing 2 has more effort but re-enters range fast", "then": "potential Phase C low; then small range = Phase D with LPS", "page": 249},
    {"id": "WA4-28", "if": "comparing up-swings", "then": "compare volume at the start of each wave first", "page": 251},
    {"id": "WA4-29", "if": "wave 3 start shows less demand while supply decreases across the structure, and Phase-B down-swings shrink", "then": "uptrend confirmed", "page": 251},
    {"id": "WA4-30", "if": "down #1 > #2 in % with dE#1>dE#2, dR#1>dR#2 and up #1 < #2 with uE#1<uE#2, uR#1<uR#2", "then": "potential accumulation range", "page": 252},
    {"id": "WA4-31", "if": "decline 2 stalls into a small Trading Range", "then": "enter on a break with large effort and large result in harmony", "page": 254},
    {"id": "WA4-32", "if": "down 2 > down 1 but immediate re-entry after committing below TR low, and up 2 > up 1 with higher effort", "then": "accumulation Trading Range", "page": 254},
    {"id": "WA4-33", "if": "price begins to imbalance", "then": "consider the nearest previous balance zone first", "page": 257},
    {"id": "WA4-34", "if": "price at VAH or VAL", "then": "expect support/resistance interaction (VA = 68.2%)", "page": 259},
    {"id": "WA4-35", "if": "VA wide vs narrow", "then": "broad participation vs little activity", "page": 260},
    {"id": "WA4-36", "if": "profile extreme tapers vs shows a high-volume block", "then": "completed auction (rejection) vs uncompleted auction (expect return)", "page": 260},
    {"id": "WA4-37", "if": "planning an upside exit from balance", "then": "require a completed auction at the lower part; else discard until tested", "page": 261},
    {"id": "WA4-38", "if": "unsure about auction completion", "then": "treat as completed", "page": 261},
    {"id": "WA4-39", "if": "price above / below VPOC", "then": "prefer Long / Short; avoid VPOC vicinity", "page": 263},
    {"id": "WA4-40", "if": "setting targets / seeking entries", "then": "HVN for targets, LVN for entries", "page": 264},
    {"id": "WA4-41", "if": "trend or Wyckoff structure in progress", "then": "Fixed Range profile to locate VPOC and VAH/VAL; act on the test", "page": 266},
    {"id": "WA4-42", "if": "several sessions' VAs overlap", "then": "one Fixed Range profile over all of them", "page": 267},
    {"id": "WA4-43", "if": "uptrend pauses", "then": "look for a combination at VPOC; first test zone above prior VA is prior VAH", "page": 268},
    {"id": "WA4-44", "if": "setting macro bias", "then": "mark LVN and HVN above and below price; long-term HVN as take-profit", "page": 269},
    {"id": "WA4-45", "if": "large LVN plus Shakeout sign; LVN near the structure", "then": "potential accumulation; expect Spring at the LVN", "page": 269},
    {"id": "WA4-46", "if": "P-shape with imbalance on the left vs right", "then": "exploitable next trend vs missed / distribution; after long downtrend warns of its end", "page": 272},
    {"id": "WA4-47", "if": "B-shape after a long uptrend", "then": "possible end of the uptrend, possible new downtrend", "page": 274},
    {"id": "WA4-48", "if": "D-shape", "then": "temporary balance; positions being built before a break", "page": 274},
    {"id": "WA4-49", "if": "structure extremes unclear", "then": "use VAH/VAL; structure limits are always LVN", "page": 275},
    {"id": "WA4-50", "if": "trading bias", "then": "follow the last HVN created; above HVN = buyers control; sell only after downward cross; no call inside HVN until break confirmed", "page": 275},
    {"id": "WA4-51", "if": "new stopping point travels shorter than the previous", "then": "momentum fell, SOT", "page": 278},
    {"id": "WA4-52", "if": "counting pushes", "then": "need at least 3; 3-4 useful; more than 4 = trend too strong", "page": 280},
    {"id": "WA4-53", "if": "momentum falls but volume large", "then": "divergence: demand appeared (decline) / supply appeared (rise)", "page": 281},
    {"id": "WA4-54", "if": "momentum falls and volume falls", "then": "exhaustion: supply drying (decline) / demand drying (rise)", "page": 282},
    {"id": "WA4-55", "if": "SOT at a Wyckoff UTAD with rising volume", "then": "expect a strong drop to the lower TR boundary; take-profit location", "page": 282},
    {"id": "WA4-56", "if": "SOT seen", "then": "confirm with a strong opposite push on large volume, then wait for the pullback to support", "page": 284},
    {"id": "WA4-57", "if": "SOT after potential Upthrust/UTAD or after potential Spring", "then": "caution on Longs / Shorts; exit on first weakness", "page": 284},
    {"id": "WA4-58", "if": "SOT at TR upper boundary vs mid-range", "then": "higher vs lower success rate; prior UTAD outranks a mid-range SOT", "page": 285},
    {"id": "WA4-59", "if": "three shrinking-range candles at an important reference", "then": "SOT in candle form; consider a sell on a clean signal", "page": 286},
    {"id": "WA4-60", "if": "impulse leg 3-4 > 1-2 / leg 5-6 < 3-4", "then": "strength / momentum weakened; weakness is not a fall, check for reversal or sideways", "page": 288},
    {"id": "WA4-61", "if": "weakness while holding a long", "then": "prepare reversal and sideways scenarios; partial profit on notable change of behaviour", "page": 289},
    {"id": "WA4-62", "if": "pullback small with weak volume / pullback larger than the previous", "then": "demand absorbing, trend continues / supply entering, partial TP 50% or 30%, close and Short on UTAD or LPSY", "page": 291}
  ]
}
```
